Experiments show that this jointly supervised median policy achieves the highest average success rates among the compared methods on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro, and two real-robot tasks, together with the shortest mean episode time among matched LIBERO baselines; code is available at https://github.com/xwangrs/Quantile-Head-for-VLA.

</details>


### [110] [FailPatch: Failure Residual Patching for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34175)
*Peng Yu,Jiacheng Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Xuchong Zhang,Baoting Li,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuxiang Chen,Tiancai Wang,Hongbin Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了FailPatch框架，通过失败驱动的残差修补，将动作监督与执行可靠性监督解耦，仅用0.52%可训练参数显著提升VLA策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通常依赖成功演示进行适配，但成功演示很少覆盖易失败状态，且缺乏纠正动作，导致部署失败难以通过传统监督学习解决。

Method: 基于动作隐藏表示在可靠与失败关联状态间具有线性可分性，在冻结VLA策略的动作隐藏空间引入Null-gated Residual Expert Bank，并采用统一的Preserve–Redirect–Trust目标，在可靠状态保留原策略、在失败关联状态选择残差专家并将表示从失败区域重定向至成功区域，实现有界干预。

Result: 仅0.52%可训练参数下，在四个长时程RoboTwin任务上成功率提升11.0个百分点，clean-to-random泛化提升9.5个百分点，在三个真实任务上较基线提升16.7个百分点。

Conclusion: FailPatch通过失败驱动的残差修补有效提升VLA策略的部署可靠性，且参数高效，兼顾可靠状态保留与失败状态纠正。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically adapted using successful demonstrations, which provide direct action supervision but rarely cover failure-prone states. Deployment failures expose these states, yet lack the corrective actions needed for conventional supervised learning. We propose FailPatch, a failure-driven residual patching framework that decouples action supervision from execution-reliability supervision. Successful demonstrations ground how the policy should act, while deployment trajectories indicate when its behavior becomes unreliable. We further observe that action hidden representations exhibit clear linear separability between reliable and failure-associated states while directly conditioning action generation. Building on these insights, FailPatch introduces a Null-gated Residual Expert Bank into the action hidden space of a frozen VLA policy. A unified Preserve--Redirect--Trust objective retains the original policy in reliable states, selects residual experts in failure-associated states and redirects representations from failure regions toward success-associated regions under bounded intervention. With only 0.52% trainable parameters, FailPatch improves success rates by 11.0 percentage points on four long-horizon RoboTwin tasks under clean evaluation, 9.5 percentage points under clean-to-random generalization, and 16.7 percentage points over the baseline across three real-world tasks. Project and code: https://github.com/yupeng-2003/FailPatch.

</details>


### [111] [WB-WAM: Heterogeneous Body-Hand Pre-training for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34199)
*Chuan Qin,Shaoting Zhu,Siyuan Luo,Siqiao Huang,Hongyu Zhao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WB-WAM 是一种世界动作模型，将显式全身动作监督融入生成式视频预训练，用于人形机器人的移动操作，实现了数据高效的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的移动操作需要身体与手部协调配合，但传统机器人预训练数据对这类全身运动覆盖有限。

Method: 提出共享物理动作空间，整合身体、根节点和灵巧手标注，利用1880.2小时部分标注的视频与运动数据进行联合视频-动作学习；通过PICO中期训练精炼先验，并用辅助前向运动学监督适配机器人任务；构建WB-Datasets支持各阶段。

Result: 仿真中HumanoidArena达到81.9%，真实世界五个任务平均成功率84.0%；任务对齐的PICO中期训练提升了下游任务表现并减少了对真机演示的需求。

Conclusion: 结果支持异构全身预训练与人体运动迁移作为实现数据高效人形移动操作的实用路径。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation demands coordinated body and hand behavior, while conventional robot pre-training data provide limited coverage of such whole-body motion. We present WB-WAM, a World Action Model that incorporates explicit whole-body action supervision into generative video pre-training. A shared physical action space integrates body, root, and dexterous hand annotations from heterogeneous sources, enabling joint video and action learning from 1880.2 hours of partially annotated video and motion data. The resulting priors are refined through PICO mid-training and adapted to robot tasks with auxiliary forward kinematics supervision. We construct WB-Datasets to support these stages with retargeted egocentric human demonstrations and robot trajectories, allowing task-aligned human motion to supplement limited robot data. Evaluations in simulation demonstrate strong whole-body task performance with 81.9% in HumanoidArena, while real-world experiments further validate WB-WAM with 84.0% mean success across five tasks. Moreover, task-aligned PICO mid-training improves downstream task performance while reducing the need for real-robot demonstrations. These results support heterogeneous whole-body pre-training and human motion transfer as a practical route to data-efficient humanoid loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [112] [Proprioceptive Force Estimation for Quadruped Locomotion and Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34222)
*Run Wang,Xu Yang,Alapati Tuerxun,Yilin Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出共享三维力估计框架，联合训练力估计器与运动策略，同时服务于负载适应与牵引引导任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动过程中需承受负载力，而牵引力可用于指定期望运动；探究能否用从本体感受历史推断的共享三维牛顿力估计同时支持这两类任务。

Method: 力估计器与运动策略联合训练，含监督力输出、速度输出与学习到的潜在上下文；估计力用于调制运动，并通过解析映射生成平面速度与偏航率指令实现牵引引导。

Result: 持续力仿真中分量力均方根误差时间均值为1.44–2.83 N；相比域随机化基线，速度跟踪与基座姿态误差分别降低21.6%和46.5%，平均存活率由68.29%提升至94.60%；Go1实验验证了静止垂直/水平力估计、8.5 kg超训练力限负载运动及牵引引导。

Conclusion: 统一的力估计接口可同时支持负载承载与牵引引导，并在仿真与真实四足机器人上得到验证。

Abstract: Payload forces must be accommodated during locomotion, while leash forces can specify desired motion. We investigate whether a shared three-dimensional force estimate in newtons, inferred from proprioceptive history under sustained loading, can support both tasks. An estimator and locomotion policy are jointly trained with supervised force and velocity outputs and learned latent context. The estimated force conditions locomotion and additionally generates planar-velocity and yaw-rate commands for leash guidance through an analytical map. In sustained-force simulation sweeps, temporal means of componentwise force root mean square error range from 1.44 to 2.83\,N. Compared with a domain-randomized baseline, the framework reduces velocity-tracking and base-orientation error scores by 21.6\% and 46.5\%, respectively, and increases mean survival from 68.29\% to 94.60\% in separate sustained-force tests. Unitree Go1 experiments demonstrate stationary vertical and horizontal force estimation, locomotion with an 8.5\,kg payload whose weight exceeds the 70\,N training force limit, and leash guidance using the same force-estimation interface.

</details>


### [113] [GAE: General Action Expert for Real-Time Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34233)
*Yuefan Wang,Huaicheng Zhou,Xiao He,Zhijie He,Mingchuan Yang,Huayi Zhang,Li Chai,Jinxin Liu,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GAE统一学习框架，实现通用、低延迟的人形机器人全身遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形化身需通过遥操作机器人扩展人类存在感，但需系统能实现多样动态的全身行为并保持人机同步的响应性。

Method: 构建大规模异构人体运动数据集；采用两阶段训练（特权生成器策略在仿真中跟踪运动并生成可行轨迹，可部署执行器策略在课程域随机化下学习跟踪）；引入延迟条件预判机制自适应补偿端到端延迟。

Result: 在Unitree G1与Westlake O1机器人上的仿真与真实实验表明，GAE能使人形机器人流畅复现多样、敏捷、富有表现力的人类行为。

Conclusion: GAE为实现通用、低延迟的人形全身遥操作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Humanoid avatars extend human physical presence beyond the body, enabling people to participate in social, service, and labor activities through remotely operated robots. This requires teleoperation systems capable of realizing diverse and dynamic whole-body behaviors while maintaining responsive human-robot synchronization. We present General Action Expert(GAE), a unified learning framework for general-purpose, low-latency humanoid whole-body teleoperation. To cover diverse human behaviors, GAE builds a large-scale human motion dataset from heterogeneous sources, including videos, animations, and motion capture, followed by standardization and augmentation. GAE then addresses the noise and embodiment mismatch in human motions with a two-stage training paradigm: a privileged generator policy first tracks human motion references in simulation and rolls out feasible humanoid trajectories; a deployable executor policy then learns to track these generated trajectories under curriculum domain randomization. For responsive human-robot synchronization, GAE introduces a latency-conditioned anticipation mechanism that adaptively compensates for end-to-end delay during real-time teleoperation. Simulation and real-world experiments on Unitree G1 and Westlake O1 robots demonstrate that GAE enables humanoids to smoothly mirror diverse, agile, and expressive human behaviors. Project website: https://wangyf0928.github.io/gae-wlrobotics/

</details>


### [114] [NavHarness: Towards Lifelong Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34276)
*Xunyi Zhao,Jian Zhou,Sihao Lin,Gengze Zhou,Zerui Li,Xinyu Yan,Jiajun Liu,Anton van den Hengel,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavHarness 是一个免训练的具身导航框架，将记忆处理融入导航循环，通过跨会话保留地图、任务记录与房屋知识，实现终身导航，在 GOAT-Bench 与 IR2R-CE 上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型在单个具身导航任务上表现良好，但在连续任务中，智能体需要依赖不断演变的地图与早期搜索记录，这些信息可能不完整或与新观测相冲突，因此需要一种能跨任务持续积累与校正经验的方法。

Method: NavHarness 为免训练的具身框架，将记忆处理作为导航循环的一部分：多轮智能体会话利用地图、任务记录与房屋知识，与观测进行核对并记录修正以指导行动；跨新任务或恢复尝试时，经验被保留在新对话中，并通过结果验证与运行结束摘要支持后续复用。

Result: 在 GOAT-Bench 上，NavHarness 相比仅依赖上下文的独立会话，s-SR 分别提升 18.6 分（Astra）与 22.6 分（Opus 5）；使用 SLAM 估计位姿时，搭配 GPT-6 Astra 达到 83.7 s-SR 与 36.9 e-SR 的最优任务成功率，并在 IR2R-CE 上达到 85.9 s-SR。

Conclusion: 跨会话的经验传递方式很关键，结构化的恢复交接优于等长摘要；在多个房屋的长期部署中，经验整合优于仅保留地图与任务记录。朝向终身导航的进展取决于连续推理会话如何建立在既有经验之上，而非仅提升单任务能力。

Abstract: Frontier models can now perform well on individual embodied navigation tasks through multi-round multimodal reasoning with simple tools. Across successive tasks, however, an agent must also rely on an evolving map and earlier search records, both of which may be incomplete or conflict with new observations. We present NavHarness, a training-free embodied harness towards lifelong navigation that makes memory processing part of the navigation loop. During navigation, its multi-round agentic session draws on maps, task records, and house knowledge, checking them against observations and recording corrections to guide its actions. NavHarness preserves this experience across fresh conversations for new tasks or recovery attempts, while outcome verification and run-end summaries support its later reuse. On GOAT-Bench, NavHarness improves s-SR over context-only independent sessions by 18.6 points with Astra and 22.6 with Opus 5. Using SLAM-estimated poses, NavHarness with GPT-6 Astra achieves state-of-the-art task success of 83.7 s-SR with 36.9 e-SR on GOAT-Bench and 85.9 s-SR on IR2R-CE. To understand these gains, we examine how experience is carried between sessions and find that structured recovery handovers outperform length-matched summaries. In extended deployments across houses, consolidation improves navigation beyond retaining maps and task records, with case studies showing how agents use earlier experience to interpret new goals, investigate unresolved questions, and resume failed searches. We suggest that progress towards lifelong navigation depends on how successive reasoning sessions build on prior experience, alongside improvements in single-task capability.

</details>


### [115] [TLC-DiT: Task-Aligned Local Visual Conditioning for Robust Multitask Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34297)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Zihang Wang,Yijun Lu,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TLC-DiT通过在DiT策略中显式引入任务引导的局部视觉特征图，提升多任务操作的成功率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件机器人策略虽在多任务操作上有进展，但任务相关的局部视觉证据通常隐藏在视觉主干或注意力层中，导致策略难以检查、在视觉变化下脆弱。这反映了缺少显式、与任务对齐的局部视觉通道。

Method: 提出TLC-DiT，作为Multitask Diffusion Transformer策略的插件式扩展，不改变扩散目标或动作生成过程。对每个相机视角，用CLIP任务嵌入通过FiLM调制冻结的DINOv2 patch特征，再经轻量CoordConv CNN适配器细化为平滑空间图，与原始全局图像、语言、关节状态和时间步条件拼接。

Result: LIBERO上平均成功率93.5%，优于Multitask DiT的86.5%和SmolVLA的79.25%；LIBERO-plus总成功率由54.07%提升至57.24%，在相机、背景和传感器噪声变化下增益更大。真实双臂任务中，Teabag Putting完成率从44%提升至89%，Match Box Opening性能相当。特征图可视化确认模型关注跨视角与扰动下的任务相关区域。

Conclusion: 显式任务引导的局部视觉通道有效提升了策略性能、鲁棒性和可解释性，提供了直接检查视觉证据的方式。

Abstract: Language-conditioned robot policies have made clear progress in multitask manipulation, but task-relevant local visual evidence usually stays hidden inside a visual backbone or attention layers. This leaves the policy difficult to inspect and fragile under visual change, two symptoms of a missing explicit, task-aligned local visual channel. We present TLC-DiT, a plug-in extension of the Multitask Diffusion Transformer (DiT) policy that adds explicit task-guided local visual feature maps without changing the diffusion objective or the action-generation process. For each camera view, frozen DINOv2 patch features are modulated by the CLIP task embedding through FiLM and refined by a lightweight CoordConv CNN adapter into smooth spatial maps, which are concatenated with the original global image, language, joint-state, and timestep conditions. On LIBERO, TLC-DiT reaches a 93.5% average success rate, compared with 86.5% for Multitask DiT and 79.25% for SmolVLA. On LIBERO-plus, the total success rate improves from 54.07% to 57.24%, with larger gains under camera, background, and sensor-noise changes. In real-world bimanual tasks, TLC-DiT raises Teabag Putting completion from 44% to 89% while maintaining comparable Match Box Opening performance. Feature-map visualizations confirm that the model attends to task-relevant regions across views and perturbations, providing a direct way to inspect the visual evidence.

</details>


### [116] [When World Models Lie: Adaptive Safety Analysis Under Wrong Imaginations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34300)
*John Cao,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应潜在安全过滤器，利用世界模型预测误差构建在线不确定性集，并通过悲观最小化进行安全评估，实现更可靠的安全推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型虽有强大的安全推理能力，但预测可能有偏差、失准或自信错误。潜在安全过滤器通常在世界模型动态上学习Hamilton-Jacobi安全值函数，当模型不准确时值函数会继承其误差，产生过度自信的安全估计。现有的辅助信号（如集成分歧、值目标一致性残差）在模型偏离观测时可能仍然很小，无法有效适应。

Method: 使用自适应保形推断（Adaptive Conformal Inference），从预测状态与观测推断的潜在状态之间的差异构建在线不确定性集，然后在这些集合上通过最小化学习到的值函数进行悲观安全评估。作者对自适应不确定性半径提供了有限时间覆盖保证。

Result: 通过仿真和硬件实验，该方法相比现有最优的潜在安全过滤器显著减少了失败情况，同时保持了任务完成度。

Conclusion: 利用直接观测到的世界模型误差来校准安全推理的自适应潜在安全过滤器，能够根据观测揭示的模型失配程度动态调整保守性，在保证安全性的同时维持任务性能。

Abstract: World models offer a powerful substrate for safety reasoning in high-dimensional robotic systems, but they are also fallible: their predictions can be biased, miscalibrated, or confidently wrong. This creates a central challenge for latent-space safety filters, which often learn Hamilton-Jacobi safety value functions on the dynamics of a world model. If the world model is incorrect, the resulting value function can inherit its errors and produce overconfident safety estimates. Existing latent safety filters often rely on auxiliary signals such as ensemble disagreement or value-target consistency residuals for adaptation, but these signals can remain small even when the world model's predictions deviate from observations. We propose an adaptive latent safety filter that calibrates safety reasoning using directly observed world-model error. Our method uses Adaptive Conformal Inference to construct online uncertainty sets from discrepancies between predicted and observation-inferred latent states, then evaluates safety pessimistically by minimizing the learned value function over these sets. This allows the filter to remain minimally conservative when the world model is accurate, while becoming more cautious when observations reveal model mismatch. We provide a finite-time coverage guarantee for the adaptive uncertainty radius. Through simulation and hardware experiments, we show that our method significantly reduces failures relative to state-of-the-art latent safety filters while preserving task completion.

</details>


### [117] [Predictive Semantic Safety: From Visual Physical Reasoning to Safety-Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34356)
*Taekyung Kim,Salem Fradi,Yanning Dai,Mateusz Ostaszewski,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性语义安全（PSS）框架，将视觉物理推理与基于备份的安全过滤相结合，通过视觉语言模型预测未来物理事件及其时序或物体位移，经共形预测校准位置误差、转换为占用区域，并对备份机动派生输入仿射约束，在MuJoCo实验中安全率显著优于仅用当前几何的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理交互会产生无法从机器人当前几何环境中察觉的未来危险，传统基于当前障碍几何的安全方法难以应对这类潜在风险。

Method: PSS框架：视觉语言模型预测物理事件及时序或直接预测物体位移；显式运动模型将事件假设转为物体轨迹；分裂共形预测在指定物体、观测时刻与未来时刻上联合校准位置误差；几何形状界将位置区域转为预测占用；据此评估规定备份机动，并导出输入仿射约束以最小修改名义输入，同时保持机器人动力学与输入限制下备份的可行性。

Result: 在MuJoCo上以Unitree Go1进行实验，涵盖坠落固定装置、冲击导致支撑失效与接触传播等场景。PSS达到99.3%的安全回合率，而仅使用当前障碍几何的备份控制障碍函数（Backup CBF）基线仅为43.3%。

Conclusion: 将视觉物理推理与基于备份的安全过滤相结合，能够有效预测未来危险并保障机器人安全，性能明显优于仅依赖当前几何的安全方法。

Abstract: Physical interactions can create future hazards that are not apparent from the robot's current geometric surroundings. We present a framework termed Predictive Semantic Safety (PSS), which connects visual physical reasoning to backup-based safety filtering. A vision-language model (VLM) predicts physical events and their timing or directly predicts object displacements. An explicit motion model converts event hypotheses into object trajectories. Split conformal prediction calibrates position errors jointly across specified objects, observation times, and future times; geometric shape bounds convert the resulting position regions into predicted object occupancy. PSS evaluates a prescribed backup maneuver against this occupancy and derives input-affine constraints for minimally modifying the nominal input while preserving backup feasibility under the robot dynamics and input limits. MuJoCo experiments with a Unitree Go1 consider falling fixtures, impact-driven support loss, and contact propagation. PSS achieves a safe episode rate of 99.3%, compared with 43.3% for a Backup Control Barrier Function baseline that only uses current obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [118] [FutureDuet: Decoupling Observation Access from Future Supervision in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34362)
*Jie Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Junqi Liu,Weichen Zhang,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Jingyan Jiang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FutureDuet 保留主视角与腕部视角双流，为两路视觉流分配不同的未来预测目标，并通过 ActionDiT 联合读取任务状态与交互状态，在 RoboTwin50 与 LIBERO 上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models 将主相机与腕部相机观测融合为单一视觉流，并用同一未来视频目标训练。但两者视觉动态差异明显：主相机稳定、反映场景级任务演进；腕相机随末端执行器运动，局部交互变化与视角移动、自遮挡混杂。这种不同的预测需求提示两路视角应使用不同的未来目标。

Method: 提出 FutureDuet：保留两路视角用于控制，但各自采用不同未来目标。主视角用未来 RGB 建模任务演进，并以交互掩码与机器人骨架将监督聚焦于任务物体与机器人运动；腕部流用未来隐变量预测建模短时程交互变化，无需像素级重建。ActionDiT 联合读取由此得到的 Task State 与 Interaction State，融合场景级进展与近距交互证据。所有辅助预测模块仅在训练阶段使用，不增加推理开销。

Result: 在 RoboTwin50 上达到 94.2% 干净场景成功率与 94.1% 随机化成功率，在 LIBERO 上平均成功率 99.2%。在六个需要精细交互的 RoboTwin50 任务上提升最显著，相比 Fast-WAM 在干净与随机化设置下平均提升 9.2% 与 12.8%。

Conclusion: 受控实验进一步表明，分离腕部通路以及为两路视角分别设计未来监督均带来互补增益，验证了差异化未来目标设计的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot action generation with future visual supervision. Existing WAMs commonly fuse main and wrist observations into one visual stream and train both with the same future-video objective, despite their different visual dynamics. A stable main camera reveals scene-level task evolution, whereas wrist cameras move with the end effector, mixing local interaction changes with viewpoint shifts and self-occlusion. These contrasting predictive demands suggest that the two views may benefit from different future objectives. We introduce FutureDuet, which retains both views for control, while allowing each visual stream to receive a different future objective. For the main view, future RGB models task evolution, while interaction masks and robot skeletons focus supervision on task objects and robot motion. For the wrist stream, future latent prediction models short-horizon interaction changes without requiring pixel-level reconstruction. ActionDiT jointly reads the resulting Task State and Interaction State, combining scene-level progress with close-range interaction evidence. All auxiliary prediction modules are training-only, adding no inference overhead. FutureDuet achieves 94.2% clean and 94.1% randomized success on RoboTwin50 and 99.2% average success on LIBERO. The improvements are most pronounced on six RoboTwin50 tasks that require precise interaction, averaging gains of 9.2% and 12.8% over Fast-WAM in clean and randomized settings. Controlled studies further show complementary gains from separating the wrist pathway and designing future supervision separately for the two views.

</details>


### [119] [RoboIRGBench: Benchmarking Implicit Referential Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34384)
*Aernaer Akelijiang,Jiannan Li,Zhineng Chen,Jingjing Chen,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究隐式指代定位（IRG）能力，提出RoboIRG-Bench基准，发现VLA模型在隐式指令下存在显著的指代鲁棒性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA基准通常假设任务相关信息在指令中被明确指定，但现实中人类常隐式指代物体、数量和关系，机器人需从语言与感知上下文中恢复意图目标，这一能力尚未被系统研究。

Method: 基于RoboMME构建RoboIRG-Bench，包含来自11个任务的40个变体，覆盖直接、推理中介、空间和上下文四类指代定位挑战；评估具有不同记忆机制的代表性VLA模型。

Result: 模型在隐式指令下性能明显下降；推理中介与空间指代尤其困难；使用外部VLM的模型更鲁棒但仍存在显著失败，替换更强VLM也无法消除缺口；在Franka Research 3机械臂上验证缺口依然存在，表现为错误指代定位与下游执行失败。

Conclusion: IRG是可靠机器人指令跟随中一个独立且尚未充分探索的能力，需发展能稳健整合语言、感知、推理与动作的VLA模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet existing benchmarks typically assume that task-relevant information is explicitly specified in the instruction. In practice, however, humans frequently refer to objects, quantities, and relations implicitly, requiring robots to recover the intended target from linguistic and perceptual context. We study this capability as Implicit Referential Grounding (IRG) and introduce RoboIRG-Bench, a manipulation benchmark designed to systematically evaluate it. Built upon RoboMME, RoboIRG-Bench contains 40 variants derived from 11 tasks and covers four challenges, including direct, reasoning-mediated, spatial, and contextual referential grounding. As IRG often requires retaining and retrieving previously established context, we evaluate representative VLAs spanning different memory mechanisms. Our evaluation reveals a noticeable referential robustness gap. Models that perform well under explicit instructions can degrade sharply when the same task-relevant information must be recovered from context. Reasoning-mediated and spatial references are particularly challenging, while models using external VLMs show greater robustness but still exhibit significant failures. Moreover, replacing the external VLM with a stronger model does not eliminate these gaps. We further validate these findings on a Franka Research 3 robot arm, where the gap persists under real-world manipulation and manifests as both incorrect referent grounding and downstream execution failures. These results establish IRG as a distinct and underexplored capability for reliable robotic instruction following and highlight the need for VLAs that can robustly integrate language, perception, reasoning, and action.

</details>


### [120] [Contact-Aware Impedance Controller for Robot-Assisted Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34400)
*MD Miraj Arefin,M Efe Tiryaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于B超图像接触感知与接触感知阻抗控制方法，实现机器人超声成像中接触点定位并降低接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助超声成像需可靠的控制器检测并定位探头-组织交互，而传统方法依赖力测量，存在残余力偏置等问题。

Method: 基于B超图像的接触感知方法，独立于力测量检测声学接触，实现接触条件下的力/力矩去偏；接触时连续估计弯曲探头表面的有效接触位置，更新控制器交互坐标系，实现物理接触点视觉伺服；并设计接触感知阻抗控制器。

Result: 琼脂仿体实验中，探头滚转角-15°至15°范围内接触定位RMSE为1.46±0.14 mm；静态滚动时保持与固定坐标系阻抗控制器相当的任务空间跟踪精度，最大压缩接触力由31.56 N降至20.09 N，降低36.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明超声图像可作为直接接触反馈，用于安全准确的机器人辅助超声成像。

Abstract: Safe robot-assisted ultrasound imaging requires a reliable controller able to detect and localize probe--tissue interaction. In this paper, we present a B-mode ultrasound image-based contact perception method and a contact-aware impedance controller for robotic ultrasound imaging. The proposed method detects acoustic contact independently of force measurements, enabling contact-conditioned force/torque taring to reduce residual wrench bias. During contact, the method continuously estimates the effective contact location along the curved probe surface and uses it to update the controller interaction frame, enabling visual servoing of the physical probe--tissue contact point during imaging. Experiments on an agar phantom demonstrated a contact-localization RMSE of $\mathbf{1.46 \pm 0.14}$~mm over probe roll angles from $\mathbf{-15^\circ}$ to $\mathbf{15^\circ}$. During static rolling, the proposed controller maintained task-space tracking accuracy comparable to a conventional fixed-frame impedance controller while reducing the maximum compressive interaction force from $\mathbf{31.56}$~N to $\mathbf{20.09}$~N, corresponding to a $\mathbf{36.3\%}$ reduction. These results demonstrate the potential of ultrasound images as direct contact feedback for safe and accurate robot-assisted ultrasound imaging.

</details>


### [121] [From Language to Task Maps: Compiling Semantic Relations While Preserving Task-Relevant Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34412)
*Jaegyun Park,Jingwang Lee,Jungsoo Lee,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出类型化的语义到几何接口，将语言关系编译为可组合的连续控制目标，并保留任务无关自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言操作指令给的是定性关系，而连续控制器需要可评估的任务量、微分和完成条件；将定性关系直接展开为完整位姿会引入非预期约束。

Method: 语言指定实体、关系和阶段，每个关系索引一个注册规格，说明任务相关区分与保留自由度；机器人端编译器用共形几何代数做grounding，构建关系特定任务映射与一致微分，并通过RMPflow组合策略。对比Semantic Topology接口与额外要求关系几何规格的接口，共60条指令；并在MuJoCo的Franka Panda上做闭环消融。

Result: 两种接口均在41/60例产生正确共享语义内容，但关键错误分别为19/60与58/60。64次grounded评估覆盖八种几何关系形式，任务映射保留注册零方向并响应关系相关扰动；解析方向导数与有限差分一致，Jacobian秩匹配注册维度。固定关系保留坐标使中位终端进度误差增加20.24–71.00 mm，而关系误差仍在评估界内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将关系可见几何与保留自由度共同编译为可组合连续目标，可更好地连接语言指令与连续控制。

Abstract: Natural-language manipulation instructions specify qualitative relations, whereas continuous controllers require state-evaluable task quantities, differentials, and completion conditions. Because a qualitative relation generally leaves part of the relative configuration unspecified, expanding it into a complete pose can introduce unintended constraints. We present a typed semantic-to-geometric interface in which language specifies entities, relations, and phases, while each relation indexes a registered specification of its task-relevant distinctions and preserved freedoms. A robot-side compiler grounds these specifications, constructs relation-specific task maps and consistent differentials using conformal geometric algebra, and composes the resulting policies through RMPflow. To evaluate the division of responsibility between the language model and the compiler, we compared a Semantic Topology interface with one that additionally requires relation-specific geometric specifications over 60 instructions. Both produced correct shared semantic content in 41/60 cases, but critical errors under their respective interface requirements occurred in 19/60 and 58/60 cases. Across 64 grounded evaluations spanning eight geometric relation forms, the task maps preserved registered null directions and responded to relation-relevant perturbations; analytic directional derivatives agreed with finite differences, and Jacobian ranks matched the registered dimensions. In three closed-loop ablations using a simulated Franka Emika Panda in MuJoCo, fixing a relation-preserved coordinate increased median terminal progress error by 20.24--71.00~mm while the retained relation errors remained within their evaluation bounds. These results support compiling relation-visible geometry and preserved freedom together into composable continuous objectives.

</details>


### [122] [From World Models to World Action Models: Rethinking Next-State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34414)
*Tingyu Yuan,Ziming Ji,Biaoliang Guan,Wen Ye,Wenrui Tian,Zhaopeng Gu,Feihong Zhang,Xu Yang,Yan Huang,Zhaowen Li,Chaoyang Zhao,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CF-WAM，一种动态下一状态预测框架，通过在不同投影（视觉、语义、几何、交互）间联合监督，提升世界-动作模型的训练效率与控制性能及跨本体泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型将下一状态固定为RGB、单一潜特征或预定义目标的静态组合，使动作学习受限于特定表示的归纳偏置，难以捕捉支撑多种未来投影的底层状态转移结构。

Method: CF-WAM动态采样同一未来的视觉、语义、几何与交互投影，统一为标准视频形式，并据此监督统一的WAM；这些投影所暴露的动作相关约束在训练步间累积，迫使模型学习动作条件下未来的共享状态转移结构，并为人与机器人学习提供跨本体动力学参照系。

Result: CF-WAM 提升了训练效率与最终控制性能，并能将人类经验有效转化为策略增益；在 RoboCasa-GR1 上平均成功率 82.50%，LIBERO-Plus 上 82.65%，真实世界评测最高达 84.00%。

Conclusion: 通过跨不同下一状态参数化的联合学习，异构的人与机器人经验可绕过外观差异、共同贡献于共享状态转移学习，从而提升跨本体泛化，并取得领先性能。

Abstract: Predicting the next state is a core paradigm of World Models for modeling physical dynamics, emphasizing prediction fidelity. As World Models evolve into World-Action Models (WAMs), existing methods still fix the next state before training as RGB, a single latent feature, or a static combination of predefined targets, thereby constraining action learning to the inductive biases preserved by a particular representation. To address this limitation, we propose CF-WAM, a dynamic next-state prediction framework that samples visual, semantic, geometric, and interaction projections of the same future, standardizes them into a common video form, and supervises a unified WAM across these projections. The action-relevant constraints exposed by these projections accumulate across training steps, forcing WAM to capture the underlying state-transition structure that supports multiple projections of the same action-conditioned future. This dynamic mechanism also provides a natural cross-embodiment dynamics reference frame for Human and Robot learning. By jointly learning across different next-state parameterizations, heterogeneous Human and Robot experience can bypass appearance differences and directly contribute to shared state-transition learning, improving cross-embodiment generalization. Experiments show that CF-WAM improves both training efficiency and final control performance, while translating Human experience effectively into policy gains. CF-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa-GR1 with an average success rate of 82.50%, while reaching 82.65% on LIBERO-Plus and up to 84.00% in real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [123] [ARS: Agentic Reward System for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34484)
*Sheng Hu,Weiyi Lu,Lingbing Zeng,Gan Weng,Weiwei Zhang,Kai Xie,Xiaofeng Mou,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ARS——一种无需额外训练、基于通用 VLM 的进度奖励建模推理框架，通过自适应视觉检查与事件提议-验证机制估计机器人任务进度，并支持外部奖励模型审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进度奖励建模需区分有意义的状态变化与失败尝试、任务无关动作，现有奖励基线在简单场景中也会对错误对象操作给出虚假进度，可靠性不足。

Method: ARS 在给定离线轨迹与任务指令下，采用自适应视觉检查进行事件提议与验证：子智能体提出任务相关事件时间线，主智能体验证修订后估计逐帧进度；可选接入终端结果标签与视觉参考，并可审计外部奖励模型的进度估计。

Result: 在 27B VLM 上，于受控语义错配基准测试中 ARS 比多个奖励基线更好地抑制虚假进度；在仿真策略学习中优于基线；并在真实机器人多螺钉紧固的长时程任务中，基于混合质量离线经验支持策略学习（全尺寸工业洗衣机装配线实验室复刻环境）。

Conclusion: 结构化推理与验证可提升通用 VLM 在机器人奖励建模中的实用性。

Abstract: Progress reward modeling is the problem of estimating how a robot's behavior changes task progress over time. Reliable estimation requires distinguishing meaningful state changes from failed attempts and task-irrelevant actions. We introduce the Agentic Reward System (ARS), an inference framework for progress reward modeling with general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs), without additional reward-model training. Given an offline trajectory and a task instruction, ARS uses adaptive visual inspection for both event proposal and verification. A subagent proposes a task-relevant event timeline, which a primary agent verifies and revises before estimating per-frame progress. ARS can incorporate optional terminal outcome labels and visual references to inform its judgments. It can also audit progress estimates from external reward models. We evaluate ARS with a 27B VLM on a controlled semantic-mismatch benchmark and downstream policy learning in simulation and on a real robot. The benchmark reveals that several evaluated reward baselines assign spurious progress to wrong-object manipulation even in simple pick-and-place scenes. ARS better suppresses these errors and outperforms these baselines in simulation policy learning. We further demonstrate that ARS supports long-horizon policy learning from mixed-quality offline experience on real-robot multi-screw fastening in a full-scale laboratory replica of an industrial washing-machine assembly line. These results suggest that structured inference and verification can improve the usefulness of general-purpose VLMs for robot reward modeling. Code is at https://github.com/midea-ai/ars

</details>


### [124] [Trajectory-Safe Orienteering for Human-Robot Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34485)
*Songqun Gao,Elena Basei,Marco Roveri,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究带时间窗与可变收益的定向问题（OPTWVP），提出两阶段“离散-连续解耦优化+服务时间引导轨迹”（DeCoST）方法，在共享空间中同时保证无碰撞轨迹与高质量任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向问题（OP）在现实与人机协作中应用广泛，但现有方法难以在共享工作空间中同时保证轨迹安全可行与任务执行质量。

Method: 提出两阶段解耦的离散-连续优化方法 DeCoST，引入面向安全的节点时间窗与离散化工作空间，以实现末端执行器与人类之间的无碰撞轨迹规划。

Result: 初步结果验证了 DeCoST 在生成无碰撞轨迹规划的同时保持定向任务质量的有效性。

Conclusion: DeCoST 能有效求解共享空间中的 OPTWVP，兼顾安全性与任务执行质量。

Abstract: Orienteering problem (OP) has wide real-world applications and also great potential in human-robot collaboration. However, existing approaches struggle to simultaneously ensure safe and feasible trajectories while achieving high-quality task execution in shared workspaces. To this end, this work studies the OP with time windows and variable profits (OPTWVP). A two-stage DEcoupled discrete-Continuous Optimization with Service-time-guided Trajectory (DeCoST) approach is proposed to effectively solve OPTWVP in shared spaces. Meanwhile, the safety-aware time windows of nodes and the discretized workspace are introduced to ensure collision-free trajectories between the end effector and the human. Preliminary results validate the effectiveness of DeCoST in generating collision-free trajectory plans while preserving the quality of orienteering tasks.

</details>


### [125] [Model-Informed Safe Reinforcement Learning for Bipedal Locomotion via Step-to-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34486)
*Victor Paredes,Ayonga Hereid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ALIP模板的模型知情强化学习框架，通过离散指数控制屏障函数提供步态安全证书，用作训练塑形信号和运行时动作过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在复杂人类环境中的部署需要原则性的安全保障。传统基于模型的步态生成器动作可解释但鲁棒性和适应性不足，而现代强化学习方法虽强但缺乏安全性保证。

Method: 以解析的角动量线性倒立摆（ALIP）模板为锚点，构建模型知情的强化学习框架。通过离散指数控制屏障函数（DECBF）为ALIP步态提供逐步安全证书，并将其用作（i）训练时的塑形信号和（ii）运行时的动作过滤器，以最小程度调整摆动足落点以满足模板级约束。

Result: 在MuJoCo中基于Digit人形机器人并配以全身控制器栈进行实证评估。与无约束基线相比，该方法在报告的外部扰动试验中减少了安全违规事件，但在较大的横向速度瞬变下暴露出安全与跟踪性能之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效降低安全违规，但安全性与运动跟踪性能之间存在权衡，尤其在大横向速度扰动情形下更为明显。

Abstract: Humanoid robots promise versatile mobility in cluttered, human-centric environments, but real deployment demands principled safety. Classical model-based gait generators yield interpretable motions but often lack the robustness and adaptability of modern reinforcement learning (RL) based approaches. We propose a model-informed reinforcement learning framework anchored to the analytical Angular Momentum Linear Inverted Pendulum (ALIP) template. We provide a step-to-step safety certificate for ALIP stepping via a discrete exponential control barrier function (DECBF) and use it as (i) a training-time shaping signal and (ii) a runtime action filter that minimally adjusts swing-foot placement to satisfy template-level constraints. Full-order safety is evaluated empirically on the Digit humanoid in MuJoCo with a whole-body controller stack. Compared to an unconstrained baseline, our approach reduces safety-violation events in the reported external-disturbance trial, while larger lateral-velocity transients reveal a safety-tracking tradeoff.

</details>


### [126] [Robot-Assisted Deployment and Maintenance of Inflatable Modules for Lunar Habitation: A Field Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34498)
*Kentaro Uno,Shamistan Karimov,Ashutosh Mishra,Elian Neppel,Hazal Gozbasi,Shreya Santra,Shinichi Kimura,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出由充气式模块与模块化可重构机器人组成的组合系统，用真实硬件（MoonBot 与 HIDAS）演示月球栖息地部署与维护任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球长期居住与原位开发需要机器人支持未来人类前哨的建设，尤其是在月球表面部署与建立居住模块方面。

Method: 采用充气式模块加模块化可重构机器人系统，使用真实硬件 MoonBot（模块化可重构机器人）与 HIDAS（充气模块）执行任务演示。

Result: 演示任务包括充气过程中的机器人巡检、模块位置对准、最终安全锁定，以及部署后维护所需的三维建图；所有演示均在实验室环境或月球模拟试验场完成。

Conclusion: 总结了经验教训，为未来月球居住场景中的机器人应用提供关键见解，以提升部署与维护的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-term human habitation and in-situ development on the Moon open a new era of space utilization. In this context, robots are a key technology for facilitating the construction of future human outposts. Toward the deployment and establishment of human habitation modules on the lunar surface, we propose a combined system consisting of inflatable modules and a modular, reconfigurable robotic system. This paper presents a report demonstrating various robot-assisted task executions using real hardware, namely the modular and reconfigurable robot MoonBot and the inflatable module HIDAS, to enhance the reliability of their deployment and maintenance. The demonstrated tasks include robotic inspection during inflation, module position alignment, final safety locking, and three-dimensional mapping for post-deployment maintenance. All demonstrations were conducted either in a laboratory environment or at a lunar analogue test site. Finally, lessons learned are discussed to provide essential insights for this robotic application to future lunar habitation.

</details>


### [127] [MonoEgo: Monocular Metric Egocentric Demonstration Capture with Passive Wrist Constellations and Sparse Workstation Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34512)
*Jie Xu,Kangjin Yu,Ziyi Jin,Beichen Wang,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoEgo 提出一种基于离线单目重建的采集系统，用被动腕部标记、工作区锚点和场景共时钟观测替代主动腕部追踪硬件，通过 MonoTag SLAM 实现度量跟踪与地图合并，实验表明可降低采集端设备需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像对齐的度量演示通常依赖专用追踪硬件与跨设备同步，采集成本高、部署复杂。作者希望用被动标记和离线单目重建减少采集侧对主动仪器的需求。

Method: 使用一台 90-FPS 全局快门相机，在同一图像时钟下观测校准的被动腕部星座、稀疏工作区锚点和场景。MonoTag SLAM 将标记角点与 ORB 几何结合，并利用视觉证据拒绝歧义的平面标记位姿；其度量 Atlas 支持区间尺度重锚定、验证式地图合并以及对早期帧的回溯定位。相机与腕部星座输出保留有效性和地图来源，不支持的运动被标记为缺失。

Result: 实验显示系统可在连续锚点可见性之外进行度量跟踪，重连受支持的地图组件，并恢复部分缺失的相机位姿。与多传感器相机参考及独立静止星座测试的比较刻画了轨迹一致性与精度，同时揭示覆盖不完整和残余几何不确定性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，被动固定装置与离线重建可降低采集侧要求；但动态精度、部署方式及下游策略收益仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Image-aligned metric demonstrations often require dedicated tracking hardware and synchronization across devices. We present MonoEgo, a capture system that replaces active wrist instrumentation with offline monocular reconstruction. One 90-FPS global-shutter camera observes calibrated passive wrist constellations, sparse workstation anchors, and the scene on a shared image clock. MonoTag SLAM combines marker corners with ORB geometry and uses visual evidence to reject ambiguous planar-marker poses. Its metric Atlas supports interval scale re-anchoring, verified map merging, and retrospective localization of earlier frames supported by the final map. Camera and wrist-constellation outputs retain validity and map provenance, and unsupported motion is left missing. Experiments show metric tracking beyond continuous anchor visibility, reconnection of supported map components, and recovery of some missing camera poses. Comparisons against a multisensor camera reference and separate stationary-constellation tests characterize trajectory agreement and precision while revealing incomplete coverage and residual geometric uncertainty. The results indicate that passive fixtures and offline reconstruction can reduce capture-side requirements. Dynamic accuracy, deployment, and downstream policy benefits require further study.

</details>


### [128] [Gaze Prompts: Temporally Dense Human Attention for Vision-Language-Action Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34550)
*Yihan Zhou,Rui Yan,Mingcong Li,Zheyuan Huang,Xu Yang,Xueyang Guo,Yilin Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"注视提示"方法，利用VR遥操作中记录的视线作为帧级视觉引导来微调VLA模型，部署时用轻量预测器替代眼动仪，六项双臂操作任务成功率从26.3%提升至56.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA微调仅提供任务级语言指令，逐步的视觉相关性是隐含的，缺少帧级的视觉引导信息。

Method: 训练时将记录的视线位置渲染为机器人头相机图像上的十字准星进行监督；部署时用轻量预测器从近期图像和指令估计注视位置，提供同类型的视觉提示，无需眼动仪也不改变策略架构。以π0为基座实现。

Result: 在六项真实双臂操作任务上，平均成功率从26.3%提升到56.0%；单策略在全部六项任务上训练时也有增益。发布了含1200条遥操作轨迹及同步视线数据的GazeMani数据集。

Conclusion: 眼动仪监督的注视提示能有效为VLA微调提供帧级视觉引导，且部署时无需额外硬件即可迁移。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) fine-tuning pairs images with actions at every step, yet typically provides only a task-level language instruction, leaving moment-to-moment visual relevance implicit. We introduce \emph{eye-tracker-supervised gaze prompting}, which uses gaze recorded during VR teleoperation to provide frame-level visual guidance for VLA fine-tuning. During training, recorded gaze locations are rendered as crosshairs on the robot's head-camera images. At deployment, a lightweight predictor estimates gaze locations from recent images and the instruction, supplying the same type of visual prompt without an eye tracker or changes to the policy architecture. Instantiated with $π_0$, gaze prompting increases mean success from $26.3\%$ to $56.0\%$ across six real-world bimanual manipulation tasks, with gains also observed when a single policy is trained on all six tasks. We release \textsc{GazeMani}, a dataset of $1{,}200$ teleoperated trajectories with synchronized gaze.

</details>


### [129] [Where Memory Belongs: Ledger, an Object Ledger for Memory-Augmented VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34554)
*Tanguy Dieudonné,Jack B. Jedlicki,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 Ledger，一种通过"记忆类型决定记忆位置"原则，将短期感知记忆置于策略内部、长期物体记忆作为外部可读记录，从而提升长程部分可观测机器人操作性能的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程、部分可观测的机器人操作需要记忆能力，但现有 VLA 模型将记忆直接嵌入策略内部，基准测试表明单一的策略内机制无法覆盖所有时空维度，与 oracle 方法差距较大。作者认为记忆类型应决定记忆的位置。

Method: Ledger 基于单个微调后的 π_{0.5} 策略，将策略内的帧采样记忆与外部时空物体记忆（ledger）配对。ledger 由 SAM3 跟踪器和 VLM 描述器从演示中构建，并由 LLM 规划器在步骤边界进行读取和决策，运行时根据指令和记录选择记忆来源，无需任务级路由器。

Result: 在 RoboMME 上，Ledger 在四套件平均分上达到 64.3%（最强先前方法为 45.9%），物体指代 60.7%（对比 40.3%），物体永久性 86.7%（对比 56.2%），且仅使用单一权重集。

Conclusion: 将短期感知记忆置于策略内、长期物体记忆作为外部显式记录的分层设计，可有效提升长程机器人操作性能，并消除对任务级路由器的需求。

Abstract: Memory is essential for long-horizon, partially observed robotic manipulation: a robot must remember which object was placed in a drawer, whose cup it moved, or how many action cycles have elapsed. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models embed memory directly inside the policy, but benchmarks show no single in-policy mechanism covers all spatio-temporal dimensions, trailing oracle methods by a wide margin. We argue that memory type dictates where memory should reside: short-term perceptual memory (repetition, timing, retracing) belongs inside the policy, while long-term object memory (persistent spatial state, containment, event history) belongs outside as an explicit, readable record. We present Ledger, a harness that realizes this split over a single fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ policy by pairing an in-policy frame-sampling memory with an external spatio-temporal object memory, the ledger, built from a SAM3 tracker and a VLM captioner of the demonstration and read by an LLM planner that decides at step boundaries. On RoboMME, Ledger reaches the highest four-suite average among the evaluated methods, 64.3% (vs. 45.9% for the strongest prior method under identical evaluation), leading object reference (60.7% vs. 40.3%) and object permanence (86.7% vs. 56.2%) using a single set of weights. Choosing the memory source at runtime, from the instruction and the record, removes the need for a task-level router.

</details>


### [130] [Aerial GRIPPER: A Gradient-based Real-time Inverse-game Predictor and Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34594)
*Zeshuai Chen,Meng Wang,Jindou Jia,Xiang Yu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GRIPPER框架，将非合作目标捕获建模为不完全信息下的广义和追逃博弈，通过在线推断目标代价参数并迭代求解开环纳什均衡，实现>50 Hz实时规划与精准抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作目标的精准捕获是极具挑战性的难题，目标行为未知且对抗性强，传统方法难以在实时性与适应性上兼顾。

Method: 将交互建模为不完全信息广义和追逃博弈；在线推断目标底层代价参数，在滚动时域循环中迭代优化开环纳什均衡策略；开发基于梯度的逆博弈求解器，利用隐式微分与快速Hessian-向量积，避免显式Hessian逆计算，实现>50 Hz更新；并设计抗扰动控制器应对不确定负载与夹爪驱动扰动。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明，GRIPPER在计算效率和任务性能上均表现优越，成功完成非合作目标的捕获与递送任务。

Conclusion: GRIPPER框架在高度对抗场景中展现出鲁棒性、适应性与实时性，验证了博弈论规划与抗扰动控制结合的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate capture of non-cooperative targets is critical. In an attempt to tackle this intractable challenge, an aerial gripper system integrated with a Gradient-based Real-time Inverse-game Predictor and PlannER (GRIPPER) framework is proposed. The interaction is formulated as a general-sum pursuit-evasion game under incomplete information. Specifically, underlying cost parameters of the target are inferred online, and the open-loop Nash equilibrium (OLNE) strategy is iteratively refined within a receding-horizon loop. To ensure high-frequency execution, a computationally friendly gradient-based inverse-game solver is developed. Without explicit computation of the Hessian inverse, the optimized solution is updated (> 50 Hz) based on implicit differentiation and fast Hessian-vector products. Meanwhile, an anti-disturbance controller is developed to overcome disturbances of uncertain payload and gripper actuation, enabling precise tracking of the planned trajectory and accurate grasping of the target. Simulations and real-world experiments illustrate the superior computational efficiency and task performance of GRIPPER. The task of capturing and delivering a non-cooperative target is accomplished, highlighting the robustness, adaptability, and real-time performance of the framework in highly adversarial scenarios.

</details>


### [131] [Efficient World Action Model Inference with Adaptive Intermediate States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34608)
*Zhinnan Liu,Haozhi Han,Ruge Zhang,Teng Ma,Tao Ma,Zheng Liu,Yifeng Chen,Yunquan Zhang,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架WAMACHINE，通过保留并自适应推理状态来加速世界动作模型（WAMs）的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs通过联合建模动作与环境动态实现前瞻控制，但迭代扩散或流式推理带来大量去噪延迟。虽然先前的推理状态提供了加速机会，但规划上下文、观测和中间表示的变化会迅速使保留状态失效，因此需要"适应"而非"直接复用"推理状态。

Method: 提出免训练框架WAMACHINE，包含三种机制：1）Trajectory Remapping：在闭环重规划间，将前一重规划状态重映射以初始化下一次重规划，减少冗余轨迹生成；2）Observation Rebinding：在去噪步间，动作执行时进行预判推理，并通过一致性检查将保留的去噪状态重绑到真实观测以继续；3）Residual Rescaling：在Transformer层间，对保留的层状态选择性重缩放，并在探针检查失败时通过中间层完整计算刷新，减少重复计算。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上对三种代表性WAM架构评估显示，观测到动作延迟加速1.47-3.05倍，每次重规划的GPU推理时间加速2.23-3.27倍，同时保留原WAM任务成功率的96.69-99.54%。

Conclusion: 自适应推理状态（而非简单复用）可在几乎不损失任务成功率的前提下显著加速WAM推理，为未来感知控制提供了高效的免训练加速方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable future-aware control by jointly modeling actions and environment dynamics. However, iterative diffusion or flow inference incurs substantial denoising latency. Prior inference state offers a natural opportunity for acceleration, yet changing planning contexts, observations, and intermediate representations can quickly render retained state stale. Preserving useful computation therefore requires adapting inference state rather than reusing it as-is. To this end, we present $\mathrm{WAM}{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ACHINE}}$, a training-free framework that accelerates WAM inference by preserving and adapting inference state for efficient and accurate continuation as the control loop evolves. Across closed-loop replans, Trajectory Remapping remaps replan state from the preceding replan to initialize the next replan, reducing redundant trajectory generation. Across denoising steps, Observation Rebinding performs anticipatory inference during action execution and rebinds retained denoising state to the real observation for continuation when consistency checks pass, reducing latency exposed to the control loop. Across Transformer layers, Residual Rescaling selectively rescales retained layer state and refreshes it through full computation of the middle layers when probe checks fail, reducing repeated Transformer computation. Evaluations of three representative WAM architectures on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 show that $\mathrm{WAM}{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ACHINE}}$ achieves 1.47-3.05$\times$ speedups in observation-to-action latency and 2.23-3.27$\times$ speedups in GPU inference time per replan, while preserving 96.69-99.54% of native WAM task success.

</details>


### [132] [On the Numerical Reliability of Differentiable Physics-Based Optimization for Robotic Material Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34666)
*Xintong Yang,Minglun Wei,Yu-Kun Lai,Ze Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究可微物理在机器人材料操作中长时程、富接触仿真梯度的数值可靠性，基于两个MPM系统辨识基准，揭示GPU归约顺序、有限差分验证与观测/损失定义对梯度与优化结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微物理被广泛用于机器人材料操作，包括系统辨识、轨迹/技能优化、示范生成及机器人或末端执行器设计，这些应用依赖长时程、富接触仿真展开中传播的梯度。因此需要考察这些梯度的数值可靠性。

Method: 采用两个源自弹塑性与颗粒材料操作的Material Point Method系统辨识基准，针对三类效应进行受控分析：GPU多对一求和的线程调度相关归约顺序；有限差分检查在长时程下的可靠性；观测与损失定义对优化行为的影响。

Result: GPU多对一求和的顺序变化会改变长时程梯度，并使一个参数梯度相对于确定性参考发生符号反转；长时程下重复运行损失波动增长远快于参数扰动引起的损失变化，使有限差分检查可靠性下降；观测与损失定义会改变优化行为及独立指标偏好的解。

Conclusion: 建议在可微仿真用于机器人优化时采用可复现的累加方式、将扰动引起的损失变化与重复运行波动进行比较的有限差分验证，并明确报告目标函数的构造。

Abstract: Differentiable physics is increasingly used in robotic material manipulation for system identification, trajectory or skill optimization, demonstration generation, and robot or end-effector design. These applications depend on gradients propagated through long, contact-rich simulation rollouts. We study the numerical reliability of those gradients using two Material Point Method (MPM) system-identification benchmarks derived from elastoplastic and granular manipulation. The benchmarks provide controlled cases for three effects that also arise in broader differentiable physics-based optimization. GPU many-to-one sums whose order depends on thread scheduling changed long-horizon gradients and reversed the sign of one parameter gradient relative to a deterministic reference. Finite-difference checks became less reliable for longer rollouts because repeated-run loss variation grew much faster than the loss change produced by the tested parameter perturbations. Observation and loss definitions changed optimization behaviour and the solution preferred by an independent metric. These results motivate reproducible accumulation, finite-difference validation that compares perturbation-induced loss changes with repeated-run variation, and explicit reporting of objective construction when differentiable simulation is used for robotic optimization.

</details>


### [133] [HOI-Retarget: Contact-Centric Retargeting for Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34674)
*Jihwan Shin,Adrià López Escoriza,Junzhe He,Matthias Heyrman,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOI-Retarget：以接触为中心的轨迹重定向方法，将人-物交互迁移到人形机器人，生成大规模动作数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LfD让人形机器人习得全身技能，但扩展到人-物交互受限于缺乏机器人可用的交互参考数据。

Method: 采用以接触为中心的重定向，把每个标定接触点作为物体坐标系中的目标，进行窗口化轨迹优化，在机器人运动学约束下平衡身体跟踪、足部支撑与平滑性。

Result: 可将单个演示增强到不同物体尺寸，接纳单目视频重建的接触，并扩展至多机器人操作同一物体。

Conclusion: 公开代码与重定向动作数据集，为人形机器人HOI学习提供大规模数据支持。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) has enabled humanoid robots to acquire diverse whole-body skills, but extending this paradigm to human-object interaction (HOI) is limited by the availability of robot-compatible interaction references. We present HOI-Retarget, a contact-centric retargeting method that transfers HOI onto a humanoid robot for large-scale motion-data generation. Its windowed trajectory optimization uses every labeled contact as a target in the object frame, balancing body tracking, foot support and smoothness under the robot's kinematic limits. The method can augment a single demonstration across object sizes, absorb contacts reconstructed from monocular video, and extend to several robots manipulating one object. We publicly release the code and the retargeted motion dataset.

</details>


### [134] [Natural State-Prediction Accuracy can Hide Weak Controlled Responsiveness in VLA Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34684)
*Hyungjoon Kim,Wonbin Son,Mi Young Lee,Jun Young Lee,Seungmin Rho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估框架，将预测精度、目标状态响应性和上下文稳定性区分开来，用于检验视觉-语言-行动（VLA）模型内部表征对物体状态的解码是否真正忠实于物理状态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从VLA模型内部表征中准确解码物体状态，并不能证明预测对目标物理状态的变化做出忠实响应。在自然观测中，物体状态、机器人构型、遮挡和任务进度共同变化，使上下文线索可能对预测产生贡献，因此需要分离这些因素进行评估。

Method: 引入一个评估框架，使用经过物理验证的观测，将目标坐标与机器人上下文交叉组合，从而分离预测精度、目标状态响应性和上下文稳定性这三种属性；并进行涉及表征、读出器和训练数据的比较与干预实验。

Result: 在固定的表征-读出器组合中，高自然轨迹精度可以与弱受控目标状态响应性共存；三种属性提供不同的诊断信息；在基于初始状态误差和物理变量的失败预测器中加入响应性和上下文敏感性，可降低新初始化下的策略失败预测误差，且同观测受控MAE也具有信息量。

Conclusion: 应同时评估目标状态响应性和上下文稳定性，而不只是自然预测精度，并进一步考察它们与实际策略行为和任务结果之间的关系。

Abstract: Accurately decoding object states from the internal representations of vision-language-action (VLA) models does not establish that the predictions respond faithfully to changes in the target physical state. In natural observations, object state, robot configuration, occlusion, and task progress vary together, allowing contextual cues to contribute to prediction. In this paper, we introduce an evaluation framework that separates prediction accuracy, target-state responsiveness, and context stability using physically validated observations that cross target coordinates with robot contexts. We demonstrate that high natural-trajectory accuracy can coexist with weak controlled target-state responsiveness in fixed representation-readout pairs. Comparisons and interventions involving representations, readouts, and training data show that the three properties provide distinct diagnostic information. Furthermore, adding responsiveness and context sensitivity to a failure predictor based on initial state error and physical variables reduces policy-failure prediction error on new initializations relative to the specified baseline while same-observation controlled MAE is also informative. These findings motivate evaluating target-state responsiveness and context stability alongside natural prediction accuracy, and examining their relationship to actual policy behavior and task outcomes.

</details>


### [135] [Sufficiency of Zeroth-Order Reward Shaping for Policy Gradient in Stabilization Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34695)
*Yisheng Zhang,Tao Wang,Sicun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人强化学习稳定控制中的奖励塑形，论证奖励无需包含一阶（速度）项，但必须对目标相关坐标达到零阶（位置）完备，速度信息应保留在策略观测中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励塑形方法多依赖经典最优控制与轨迹优化的启发式原则，且未区分控制目标固有的奖励项与数值正则项，导致超参数调试脆弱、缺乏原则性指导。

Method: 以稳定控制问题为研究对象，从零阶（构型）与一阶（速度）信息入手，通过理论分析与实证实验对比策略梯度方法在有/无一阶奖励项下的表现，并考察目标相关坐标的零阶完备性与一阶状态在策略观测中的必要性。

Result: 策略梯度方法在没有一阶奖励项时也能成功求解稳定任务；加入一阶奖励项反而在其尺度增大时引入严重敏感性；奖励函数必须在目标相关坐标上零阶完备，而一阶状态在低耗散假设下仍需保留在策略观测中。

Conclusion: 研究为机器人强化学习的奖励设计提供了可操作且具原则性的指导：奖励应保证目标相关坐标的零阶完备性，无需人为加入一阶速度奖励项，速度信息交由策略观测处理。

Abstract: Reward shaping is fundamental to modern robotic control with deep reinforcement learning (RL), yet practitioners still rely heavily on heuristic principles borrowed from classical optimal control and trajectory optimization. Existing methods rarely distinguish reward terms that are intrinsic to the control objective from numerical regularizers, leading to brittle hyperparameter tuning. To determine which quantities a reward must contain, we study the stabilization control problem with a focus on zeroth-order (configuration) and first-order (velocity) information. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that policy gradient methods can successfully solve stabilization tasks without first-order reward terms, adding such terms can instead introduce severe sensitivity as their scale grows. Conversely, our findings confirm that reward functions must be zeroth-order complete over goal-relevant coordinates, while the first-order state remains necessary in the policy observation under our low-dissipation assumptions. Overall, these results provide actionable and principled guidance for reward design in robotic RL.

</details>


### [136] [MarsLab: A Martian Rover Simulator for Planetary Rover Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34702)
*Hoyun Kim,Beomsu Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MarsLab是一个开源、ROS2原生的火星车仿真器，基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建，用于自主导航算法开发，并提供SLAM与VPR基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来火星任务需要火星车在非结构化地形、光照变化、大气尘埃和有限通信条件下具备自主能力。仿真是在部署前研究这些条件的实用手段，但现有火星相关资源（任务型仿真器、固定模拟数据集、任务专用环境、开放机器人接口）在范围上各不相同，缺乏统一平台。

Method: MarsLab结合HiRISE数据与程序化生成地形，提供可定制的岩石、陨石坑、太阳光照和大气尘埃设置，在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中运行Perseverance级火星车模型；运行时通过标准ROS2话题发布RGB、深度、RGB-D点云、LiDAR、IMU、轮式里程计和真值位姿数据。

Result: 论文展示了跨传感模态、尘埃水平、场景几何和路径长度的SLAM基准测试，以及在光照与尘埃变化下重复火星基地穿越的VPR基准测试。结果表明，受控场景变化与共享真值轨迹可用于在同一仿真器内比较轨迹级估计与图像级地点识别。

Conclusion: MarsLab提供了统一的开源仿真平台，支持在可控条件下对比SLAM与VPR等自主导航算法，为火星车自主性研究提供了可复现的基准测试环境。

Abstract: Future Mars missions will require rover autonomy that can operate across unstructured terrain, changing illumination, atmospheric dust, and limited communication. Simulation is a practical way to study these conditions before deployment, but existing Mars-relevant resources differ in scope, including mission-oriented simulators, fixed analog datasets, task-specific environments, and open robotics interfaces. In this context, we present MarsLab, an open-source, ROS2-native Mars rover simulator for autonomy and navigation algorithm development. MarsLab combines HiRISE-derived and procedural terrain with customizable rock, crater, solar-illumination, and atmospheric-dust settings, and runs a Perseverance-class rover model in NVIDIA Isaac Sim. The runtime publishes RGB, depth, RGB-D point clouds, LiDAR, IMU, wheel odometry, and Ground Truth (GT) pose data through standard ROS2 topics. We demonstrate MarsLab with Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) benchmarks across sensing modalities, dust levels, scene geometry, and route length, and with Visual Place Recognition (VPR) benchmarks over repeated Mars Base traversals under illumination and dust changes. The results illustrate how controlled scene variation and shared GT trajectories can be used to compare trajectory-level estimation and image-level place recognition within the same simulator. Our Project Page: https://kimhoyun-robotair.github.io/MarsLab/.

</details>


### [137] [SOR-Nav: Search or Relocate? Context-Gated Exploration and Cross-Region Relocation for Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34707)
*Yuan Ji,Zirui Li,Yuxin Cai,Shuge Wu,Boon Siew Han,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SOR-Nav分层导航系统，通过在当前上下文继续探索与跨区域迁移之间显式仲裁，在HM3D和MP3D上取得最优SR与SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体导航方法主要通过排序候选目标点来优化"下一步去哪"，缺乏对"是否应放弃当前搜索上下文、转向更有希望的可行区域"的显式决策。

Method: 构建自主语义探索系统，累积持久3D物体簇并将可行前沿组织为簇决策图；使用上下文门控的LLM驱动物体搜索监督器评估当前搜索上下文适宜性，决定继续探索或进行跨区域迁移。

Result: 在HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2、MP3D完整验证集上均取得最强SR和SPL；MP3D上SPL从18.1%提升至38.5%（翻倍以上），SR从50.7%提升至61.8%。

Conclusion: 消融实验验证了所提决策结构的有效性，真实世界三目标连续物理部署证明了系统的持续ObjectNav运行能力。

Abstract: Object navigation requires an embodied agent to find an object in an unseen environment under partial observability and a limited motion budget. Existing methods primarily optimize where the robot should go next by ranking candidate destinations. In contrast to these methods, we present SOR-Nav, a hierarchical navigation system that explicitly arbitrates between continuing to explore the current context and abandoning it for a more promising reachable region. First, an autonomous semantic exploration system is built that accumulates persistent 3D object clusters and organizes reachable frontiers into a cluster decision graph to provide an efficient search abstraction. Then, SOR-Nav uses a context-gated LLM-driven object-search supervisor to evaluate the suitability of the current search context and decide whether to continue exploration or perform cross-region relocation to another reachable frontier cluster. Across the complete, unfiltered validation sets of HM3D-v1, HM3D-v2, and MP3D, SOR-Nav achieves the strongest reported Success Rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) on all three benchmarks. On MP3D in particular, it more than doubles the previous best SPL from 18.1\% to 38.5\% while increasing SR from 50.7\% to 61.8\%. Nested HM3D-v2 ablations validate the proposed decision structure, while a continuous three-target physical deployment demonstrates persistent ObjectNav operation in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [138] [ExcavaTwin: Training-Free Geometry-Guided Semantic Elevation Mapping for Autonomous Excavation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34719)
*Yu Deng,Lingshan Zeng,Tong Hu,Rushi Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExcavaTwin：一种无需挖掘专用训练的纯视觉、几何引导语义高程建图框架，融合冻结视觉模型的多视图几何与语义，生成面向任务的语义高程图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主挖掘需要同时刻画地形几何与任务相关语义的空间表示；现有挖掘建图多偏重高程，而通用语义模型在非结构化室外场景中表现不稳定。

Method: 输入多视图RGB图像，四步流程：1）用冻结视觉模型重建场景几何与语义观测；2）推导地形与非地形的几何支撑；3）进行几何约束的多视图语义融合，抑制不合理预测并补全缺失观测；4）将融合状态投影为面向任务的语义高程图。

Result: 在公开数据集与真实挖掘场景中实现可靠的几何与语义感知；真实挖掘中平均更新间隔约1.4秒，动态修改区域平均高程误差12.74厘米；较大误差主要出现在地形快速变化与机器运动引起的瞬态视觉干扰时。

Conclusion: 该纯视觉、几何引导的框架无需挖掘专用训练即可构建语义高程图，具备实际挖掘场景中的可用性与实时性，后续需应对快速地形变化与运动视觉干扰带来的误差。

Abstract: Autonomous excavation requires a spatial representation that jointly captures terrain geometry and task-relevant semantics. Existing excavation mapping is largely elevation-centric, while generic semantic models remain unstable in unstructured outdoor scenes. We present ExcavaTwin, a pure-vision geometry-guided semantic elevation mapping framework without excavation-specific training. Given multi-view RGB images, the framework: 1) reconstructs scene geometry and semantic observations using frozen vision models; 2) derives terrain and non-terrain geometric support; 3) performs geometry-constrained multi-view semantic fusion to suppress implausible predictions and recover incomplete observations; and 4) projects the fused state into a task-oriented semantic elevation map. Experiments on public datasets and real excavation scenes demonstrate reliable geometric and semantic perception. In real excavation, the system achieved an average update interval of approximately 1.4 s and a mean elevation error of 12.74cm in dynamically modified regions. Larger errors mainly occur during rapid terrain changes and transient visual disturbances caused by machine motion.

</details>


### [139] [DexWeave: Learning Dexterous Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34724)
*Naichuan Sun,Haotian Shen,Yizhang Zhang,Luying Feng,Haoze Wang,Yuanbo Xiangli,Yaochu Jin,Peidong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexWeave是一个统一框架，将交互一致的运动重定向与解剖结构感知的全身策略学习相连接，实现人形机器人灵巧全身移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类演示学习灵巧人形移动操作，不仅要迁移人类运动，还要迁移其协调的交互结构。但不同实体差异会破坏身体运动、手腕位置、手指关节与物体交互之间的耦合，而运动学上准确的参考在机器人动力学下仍可能难以实现。

Method: 提出两阶段重定向：先用专门求解器初始化身体与手部运动，再在上肢交互链上进行耦合细化并保持下肢支撑；随后用解剖结构感知的Transformer策略跟踪参考，将解剖区域表示为结构化token，通过有向掩码注意力建模依赖，物体信息选择性调节上肢通路；策略联合输出身体与灵巧手动作，直接用强化学习训练，无需预训练跟踪策略、师生蒸馏或残差细化。

Result: 相比MLP基线，DexWeave提升了重定向保真度与交互一致性，取得了更高的操作性能和更快的策略收敛；在配备Inspire灵巧手的Unitree G1实体人形上部署，实现了真实世界的灵巧全身移动操作。

Conclusion: DexWeave通过交互一致重定向与解剖感知全身策略学习的统一，实现了从人类演示到真实人形机器人的灵巧全身移动操作。

Abstract: Learning dexterous humanoid loco-manipulation from human demonstrations requires transferring not only human motion, but also the coordinated interaction structure underlying the demonstrated behavior. This is challenging because embodiment differences distort the coupling among body motion, wrist placement, finger articulation, and object interaction, while kinematically accurate references may still be difficult to realize under robot dynamics. We present DexWeave, a unified framework that connects interaction-consistent motion retargeting with anatomy-aware whole-body policy learning. DexWeave first employs a two-stage retargeting procedure that initializes body and hand motions with specialized solvers and subsequently performs coupled refinement over the upper-body interaction chain while preserving lower-body support. The resulting references are tracked by an anatomy-aware Transformer policy that represents anatomical regions as structured tokens and uses directed masked attention to model their dependencies, with object information selectively conditioning the upper-body pathway for dexterous interaction. The policy jointly outputs body and dexterous-hand actions and is trained directly with reinforcement learning, without pretrained tracking policies, teacher-student distillation, or subsequent residual refinement. DexWeave improves retargeting fidelity and interaction consistency while achieving higher manipulation performance and faster policy convergence than MLP baselines. We further deploy the learned policies on a physical Unitree G1 humanoid equipped with Inspire dexterous hands, demonstrating dexterous whole-body loco-manipulation in the real world. See our project page (https://dexweave.github.io) for videos.

</details>


### [140] [Action Sequence Transfer via LLMs for Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34730)
*Choongho Chung,DongHwan Shin,Sung-Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种动作序列迁移系统，可将源空间中的用户动作序列自适应迁移到目标空间，在物体配置差异较大的场景间仍能生成有效动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户动作序列通常与特定环境的物体和空间约束紧密相关，直接迁移到新环境往往不适用，因此需要一种能适应目标空间约束的动作序列迁移方法。

Method: 输入源空间的用户动作序列以及源、目标环境的两套场景图表示，系统利用多层级用户活动表示，在不同抽象层次上对动作进行泛化，从而预测出符合目标空间空间与物体约束的对应动作序列；并基于Ego4D GoalStep数据集构建了新的场景图数据集用于评估。

Result: 实验表明，即使在物体配置差异极大的源空间与目标空间之间，系统也能生成有效的动作序列。

Conclusion: 通过场景图与多层级动作抽象表示，动作序列可被有效迁移至新环境，为跨空间的活动迁移提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present an action sequence transfer system that adaptively transfers user action sequences across different target spaces. Given an input action sequence from a source space and scene graph representations of both the source and target environments, our system predicts a corresponding action sequence in the target space by adapting to the spatial and object constraints of the new environment. To achieve this, we leverage multi-level representations of user activity to generalize actions at varying levels of abstraction. To demonstrate our system, we collect a new scene graph-based dataset derived from the Ego4D GoalStep dataset for evaluation. Results indicate that our system can generate valid action sequences even between spaces with drastically different object configurations.

</details>


### [141] [Simulation for Planetary Robotic Perception and Autonomy: A Concise Survey of Recent Capabilities and Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34743)
*Hoyun Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于行星机器人仿真工作的综述，围绕四个实用维度对现有仿真工作进行梳理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星机器人系统的开发与验证困难重重，因为代表性实地测试成本高、机会有限且在任务相关条件下往往不可重复。仿真因此成为感知与自主研究、合成数据生成、系统集成和部署前评估的核心工具，但相关文献分散于不同仿真引擎、实现方式与应用场景中，缺乏系统梳理。

Method: 本文对面向行星机器人感知与自主的仿真工作进行综述，按四个实用维度组织：开放性与可获取性、场景与平台覆盖范围、传感器与感知支持、环境与操作真实性。

Result: 所综述的仿真工作均报告了视觉或物理保真度，并支持面向感知的工作流。同时显示出若干不足：公开可获取性不均衡、以漫游车为中心的覆盖偏向、对专用传感模态的支持不完整，以及对星载计算、能量与通信限制等操作约束的报告不统一。

Conclusion: 现有行星机器人仿真文献在感知与自主研究方面已有较多积累并提供保真度报告，但在开放共享、平台多样性、专用传感器支持及操作约束刻画方面仍存在明显缺口，值得未来工作改进。

Abstract: Planetary robotics is an important enabler of scientific exploration in environments where direct human-in-the-loop operation is costly, hazardous, or infeasible. However, developing and validating planetary robotic systems remains difficult because representative field testing is expensive, limited, and often unrepeatable under mission-relevant conditions. In this setting, simulation serves as a central tool for perception and autonomy research, synthetic data generation, system integration, and pre-deployment evaluation. Despite its importance, the literature on planetary robotics simulation remains dispersed across different simulation engines, implementations, and application settings. This paper surveys simulation works for planetary robotic perception and autonomy across four practical axes: Openness and Availability, Scenario and Platform Coverage, Sensor and Perception Support, and Environmental and Operational Realism. The surveyed simulation works report visual or physical fidelity and support perception-oriented workflows. They also indicate uneven public availability, rover-centered coverage, partial support for specialized sensing modalities, and uneven reporting of operational constraints such as onboard computation, energy, and communication restrictions.

</details>


### [142] [CoHuB: A Simulation Benchmark for Multi-Humanoid Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34782)
*Hyunjin Park,Jebeom Chae,Minwoo Park,Sunghyun Park,Hanjun Yoo,Seoyeon Choi,Soochul Yoo,Joohwan Seo,Sarmad Idrees,Jae-Sang Hyun,Jongmin Lee,Roberto Horowitz,Youngwoon Lee,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoHuB：面向自我中心视觉下多类人机器人协作的仿真基准，含10项任务与VR遥操作演示，评估多机器人协作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类环境中的许多物理任务需要协作，但现有人形机器人基准多聚焦单机器人技能，缺乏在自我中心视觉观测下多机器人协作的评估。

Method: 提出CoHuB仿真基准，包含10项任务（8项双机器人、2项三机器人），覆盖多种协作模式；通过多操作员VR遥操作流水线采集同步演示，每名操作员从各自机器人的自我中心视角控制一台机器人。

Result: 在代表性视觉运动策略上的实验显示出在协调感知与控制方面存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: CoHuB为开发与评估多类人机器人协作策略提供了基础。

Abstract: Many physical tasks in human environments require collaboration, from assisting a partner to jointly manipulating an object. Yet, existing humanoid benchmarks largely focus on single-humanoid skills and lack evaluation of multi-humanoid collaboration under egocentric visual observations. We introduce CoHuB (Collaborative Multi-Humanoid Benchmark), a simulation benchmark for multi-humanoid collaboration under egocentric visual observations. CoHuB provides 10 tasks, eight with two humanoids and two with three humanoids, spanning diverse collaboration patterns. We also provide synchronized demonstrations collected through a multi-operator VR teleoperation pipeline, in which each operator controls one humanoid from its egocentric view. Experiments with representative visuomotor policies reveal substantial challenges across different forms of coordinated perception and control. CoHuB provides a foundation for developing and evaluating multi-humanoid collaboration policies.

</details>


### [143] [AGRO-SUVIDE: Agentic Robotics for Surgical Viscoelastic Debridement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34823)
*Shutong Jin,Ziyang Chen,Preethi Satish,Medow Shen,Gary Guthart,Florian T. Pokorny,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向手术黏弹性清创（反复清除黏性基底上小碎片）的智能体机器人框架 AGRO-SUVIDE，在 dVRK 上平均单片清除成功率达 85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术中的重复性操作（如反复清除附着于黏弹性基底的小碎片）容易造成术者疲劳，增强灵巧性有望缓解这一问题；而现有方法缺乏面向该类黏弹性清创任务的自动化框架。

Method: 采用模块化智能体框架，利用智能体的自我改进与编码能力：演示分析模块从单次专家演示中结合视觉与运动学信息自动识别可复用技能；构建模块将每个技能实现为基于过程模型的技能（智能体基于脚手架库编写代码）或无模型策略技能；运行时的监控模块依据观测到的碎片数量将技能组合成循环式图，并校验每个技能的前置与后置条件以决定推进或重试。

Result: 在 da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) 上完成 340 次物理试验：单碎片清除平均成功率 85%；连续三碎片清除成功率 60%，加入一次人工干预后达 95%；并可泛化到未见过的五碎片场景，单碎片清除平均成功率 80%。

Conclusion: AGRO-SUVIDE 表明基于智能体的模块化技能学习与组合框架可有效实现黏弹性清创中的重复性碎片清除，并在多碎片与未见过场景下具备一定泛化能力。

Abstract: Augmented dexterity has the potential to reduce the fatigue experienced by surgeons during repetitive surgical tasks. In this paper, we propose the first AGentic RObotics framework for SUrgical VIscoelastic DEbridement (AGRO-SUVIDE), the repeated removal of small fragments attached to a viscoelastic substrate. Leveraging the self-improving and coding capability of agents, AGRO-SUVIDE adopts a modular framework. Specifically, the demonstration analysis module automatically identifies recurring skills from a single expert demonstration, using both visual and kinematic information. The construction module then builds each skill, either as a procedural model-based skill the agent codes against a scaffolded library or as a model-free policy-based skill. At runtime, the monitoring module composes the skills into a loop-style graph sized to the number of fragments it observes, then verifies pre- and post-conditions of each skill to decide whether to advance or retry. We evaluate AGRO-SUVIDE through 340 physical trials on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). AGRO-SUVIDE achieves an average single-fragment removal success rate of 85%, completing consecutive three-fragment removal at 60% and at 95% with one human intervention. It further generalizes to unseen five-fragment scenarios with an average success rate of 80% for single-fragment removal. Project page: https://surgical-robotics.github.io/AGRO-SUVIDE/

</details>


### [144] [Don't Throw Away the Tail: Action Upcycling for Policy Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34911)
*Taesung Kwon,Jangho Park,Sunwoo Park,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的 Action Upcycling 算法，通过复用被丢弃的动作，在平滑性允许范围内延长执行时域，减少策略调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人策略从单次观测预测动作块，只执行前缀后丢弃其余动作再重规划。执行时域的选择存在反应性与效率的权衡：短时域保持反应性但需频繁调用策略。现有测试时自适应方法要么读取模型内部（信号需按架构选择），要么额外采样（增加成本）。因此需要一种无需访问内部、无需额外采样、可通用的时域选择方法。

Method: Action Upcycling：一种免训练算法，复用策略原本会丢弃的动作。核心发现是只要动作速度保持平滑，被丢弃的动作与其重规划版本保持接近。因此算法将执行时域延长至速度开始波动的临界点。不访问模型内部，也不额外采样。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务上，跨多种 Vision-Language-Action 模型（VLA）乃至 World Action Model（WAM）的广泛实验表明，可在成功率无损的情况下将策略调用次数减少 1.2–1.7 倍。

Conclusion: Action Upcycling 以可忽略成本适用于任意分块策略，且与其他加速方法（少步采样、流式动作解码）正交，为策略加速开辟了新维度。

Abstract: Modern robot policies predict a chunk of future actions from a single observation, execute only a prefix, and discard the rest before replanning. Choosing the length of this prefix, the execution horizon, poses a trade-off between reactivity and efficiency. A short horizon keeps the policy reactive to the environment, but requires frequent policy calls. Recent test-time methods adaptively select the horizon for each chunk, but they either read model internals, where the signal must be chosen for each architecture, or draw extra samples, which adds cost. We propose *Action Upcycling*, a training-free algorithm that reuses actions the policy would otherwise discard, without accessing model internals or drawing extra samples. We find that discarded actions stay close to their replanned versions as long as the action velocity remains smooth. Action Upcycling therefore extends the execution horizon up to the point where the velocity begins to fluctuate. Extensive experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation tasks show that Action Upcycling reduces policy calls by 1.2--1.7$\times$ with no loss in success rate, across multiple Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) and even a World Action Model (WAM). It applies to any chunked policy at negligible cost and is orthogonal to other policy acceleration methods such as few-step sampling and streaming action decoding, opening a new axis for policy acceleration.

</details>


### [145] [RoboFL: Federated Expert Assembly for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34968)
*Rongyu Zhang,Ruizhi Fan,Yunfan Lou,Hengyu Fang,Shenli Zheng,Chenrui Wu,Yili Jin,Li Du,Dan Wang,Yuan Du,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFL 将本地训练的 LoRA 适配器作为服务器 MoE 的专家分支，通过 MoSAIC、FARD 与 PCEA 实现联邦世界-动作学习，在提升性能的同时大幅降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作与世界-动作模型受限于物理交互数据的稀缺、机构间数据孤岛以及任务异构性。联邦学习结合参数高效微调是自然解法，但直接聚合适配器会缠绕不兼容更新，而引入 MoE 式路由又可能稀释专业性并造成专家选择不稳定。

Method: 提出 RoboFL，实例化 MoSAIC（槽位适配器混合）：将本地训练的 LoRA 适配器直接安装为服务器 MoE 的专家分支；服务器路由器在这些先验分支上学习 token 分配并联合优化路由与专家参数。FARD（前瞻到动作路由蒸馏）对齐模型三条路径的路由，PCEA（路径共识专家聚合）将完整专家更新压缩为紧凑全局适配器用于个性化再分发。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、RLBench 及真实 Franka 机械臂上验证，结构化专家组装使 RoboFL 在 Franka 上比集中式 PEFT InternVLA-A1 高 12.23%，同时相较基于 MoE 的联邦 VLA 基线将每轮客户端通信降低最多 86.81%。

Conclusion: RoboFL 通过将本地 LoRA 适配器结构化组装为服务器 MoE 专家并配合路由蒸馏与共识聚合，在联邦世界-动作学习中同时实现了性能提升与通信效率优化。

Abstract: Vision-language-action and world-action models are increasingly popular, yet remain bottlenecked by physical interaction data that is scarce, institutionally siloed, and task-heterogeneous. A natural federated solution is to let each client adapt a shared foundation model through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, avoiding the exchange of full-model updates. However, federating these adapters is nontrivial, as naive aggregation can entangle incompatible updates, while incorporating MoE-style routing into federated aggregation may dilute specialization and destabilize expert selection. We present RoboFL, which instantiates MoSAIC (Mixture of Slotted Adapters) for federated world-action learning. MoSAIC directly installs locally trained LoRA adapters as the expert branches of a server MoE. Server-side routers learn token assignments over these prior-informed branches while jointly refining routing and expert parameters. Foresight-to-Action Routing Distillation (FARD) aligns routing across the model's three paths, while Path-Consensus Expert Aggregation (PCEA) converts complete expert updates into a compact global adapter for personalized redistribution. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0, RLBench, and a real-world Franka robot arm show the superiority of RoboFL with structured expert assembly, as it outperforms centralized PEFT InternVLA-A1 by 12.23% on the Franka arm, while reducing per-round client communication by up to 86.81% relative to MoE-based federated VLA baselines.

</details>


### [146] [NavJev: Efficient Vision-Language Navigation via Action-Centric Visual Compression and Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34969)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Jinlong Li,Haojie Dai,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavJev 将在线导航从重复的多模态生成重构为视觉压缩加轻量类型化动作选择，在 R2R-CE 上以每步 0.65 秒达到 27.0% SR 与 22.4% SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本视觉语言导航方法依赖多模态大模型在每一步进行自回归多模态推理，虽有效但推理延迟高，限制具身智能体的响应能力。

Method: 提出 NavJev 框架：Action-Centric Visual Compression（ACVC）将航点几何、BLIP 描述与 RAM 语义标签整合为候选动作的紧凑表示；Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory（DASM）过滤共享语义并维护跨导航步的判别性动作特定证据；据此直接对可用动作集做结构化概率决策。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 上取得 27.0% SR 与 22.4% SPL，每导航步仅需 0.65 秒，相比基于 MLLM 的 VLN 方法显著降低推理延迟与成本。

Conclusion: NavJev 通过将多模态推理替换为紧凑视觉压缩与轻量动作选择，在保持导航性能的同时大幅提升效率，为高效零样本 VLN 提供可行路径。

Abstract: Recent zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) methods increasingly rely on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to reason over visual observations, navigation instructions, and candidate actions. Although effective, repeatedly invoking autoregressive multimodal reasoning at every navigation step introduces substantial inference latency, limiting the responsiveness of embodied agents. We propose NavJev, an efficient VLN framework that reformulates online navigation from repeated multimodal generation into compact visual compression followed by lightweight typed action selection. Specifically, Action-Centric Visual Compression (ACVC) integrates waypoint geometry, BLIP captions, and RAM semantic tags into compact representations of candidate actions, while Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory (DASM) filters shared semantics and maintains discriminative action-specific evidence across navigation steps. Based on these representations, Jev directly performs structured probabilistic decisions over the available action set. Experiments on R2R-CE show that NavJev achieves 27.0% SR and 22.4% SPL with only 0.65 s per navigation step, while substantially reducing inference latency and cost compared with MLLM-based VLN methods. The project page is available at https://kai-sheng-caesar.github.io/NavJev/.

</details>


### [147] [Graph-Based Simultaneous Path and Foothold Planning for Multi-Limbed Intra-Vehicular Robots in Space Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35000)
*Masazumi Imai,Kentaro Uno,Toshinori Kuwahara,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图论的多肢舱内机器人（MLIVR）路径与落脚点同步规划框架，用于空间站内部作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间站机器人作业可减轻航天员负担、提升在轨活动效率；多肢舱内机器人借助抓取末端可抓握手扶栏、座椅轨道等既有接口，实现微重力下的稳定移动与操作，但这些接口上可抓取位置空间受限且离散分布，因此运动规划必须与落脚点规划联合处理。

Method: 基于图论构建路径与落脚点同步规划框架，在满足可操作度约束的前提下高效搜索多肢机器人可行的支撑序列（stance sequences）。

Result: 在国际空间站（ISS）舱室3D模型中进行仿真验证，结果表明该框架能够在真实舱内环境中生成可行且高效的移动规划。

Conclusion: 所提同步路径与落脚点规划框架对多肢舱内机器人在空间站内的移动规划有效，具备实际舱内环境下的可行性。

Abstract: Robot-aided operations in space stations are essential for reducing the workload of astronauts and improving the efficiency of on-orbit activities.
