Multi-limbed intra-vehicular robots (MLIVRs) equipped with grappling end-effectors have emerged as a promising solution, as they can securely grasp pre-existing interfaces, such as handrails and seat tracks, thereby enabling stable locomotion and forceful manipulation in microgravity environments. Since graspable locations on these interfaces are spatially limited and discretely distributed, motion planning for MLIVRs must be addressed jointly with foothold planning. This paper presents a simultaneous path and foothold planning framework based on graph theory for MLIVRs. The proposed method efficiently searches for feasible stance sequences for a multi-limbed robot while satisfying manipulability constraints. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated through simulations in a 3D model of the International Space Station (ISS) cabin, demonstrating its capability to generate feasible and efficient locomotion plans in realistic intra-vehicular environments.

</details>


### [148] [Learning to Act under Visual Interruptions with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35003)
*Mingle Jiang,Rui Xu,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究VLA模型在相机中断时的鲁棒性问题，提出MAIL-Bench基准和MINT方法，显著提升相机丢失下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常在任务执行全程所有相机流可用的假设下开发与评测。当某相机在任务中途停止提供画面时，策略必须在缺失该视角后续观测的情况下继续动作。尽管这一问题具有重要现实意义，但此类中断对闭环操作的影响尚缺乏充分理解。

Method: 论文提出MAIL-Bench基准，通过在每个策略成功参考轨迹的多个阶段中断不同相机，衡量策略在视觉输入不可用时的能力保持情况。在此基础上提出MINT方法：先训练VLA策略在视觉输入缺失下仍能运作；推理时利用光流外推或动作条件世界模型选择性地补充缺失观测，并在预测视角不可靠时撤回该预测。

Result: 在π_{0.5}和GR00T N1.5上的实验表明，相比原始模型，MINT在相机丢失情况下显著提升任务成功率。在AgiBot G2上的实验进一步验证了相机丢失下的真实机器人部署效果。

Conclusion: MAIL-Bench揭示了视觉中断对闭环操作的影响，MINT通过训练鲁棒策略与推理时选择性观测补充有效缓解相机丢失问题，并在仿真与真机上得到验证。基准已公开。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, but they are typically developed and evaluated with all camera streams available throughout task execution. When a camera stops delivering frames during task execution, the policy must continue acting without access to subsequent observations from the missing view. Despite its practical importance, how such interruptions affect closed-loop manipulation remains insufficiently understood. To investigate this problem, we introduce MAIL-Bench, a benchmark that evaluates visual interruptions with VLA models. By interrupting different cameras at multiple stages of each policy's successful reference trajectory, MAIL-Bench measures how well policies retain their capabilities when visual inputs become unavailable. Building on this benchmark, we propose MINT, which first trains VLA policies to remain functional under missing visual inputs. At inference time, MINT selectively supplements missing observations using optical-flow extrapolation or an action-conditioned world model, and withdraws predicted views when they become unreliable. Experiments on $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 show that MINT significantly improves task success under camera loss over the original models. Experiments on AgiBot G2 further demonstrate the real-robot deployment under camera loss. The benchmark is available at https://minglejiang.github.io/Mail-Bench/

</details>


### [149] [Do Not Cut When Uncertain: Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads for VLA Policies in Robotic Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35039)
*Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA策略因输出接口无法表达"我不知道/不应行动"而在遮挡下产生高置信度错误；本文提出可拒绝、标定的决策头RCDH，冻结VLA骨干即可提升遮挡下的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA经行为克隆或流匹配训练后必须输出动作轨迹，无法表达拒答或拒绝行动。在机器人采摘中，遮挡使单帧决策本质歧义：相同像素可能对应可切割茎秆，也可能根本没有茎秆。VLA被迫"表态"，导致高置信度且不可逆的错误。作者认为VLA的失败模式由输出接口而非骨干规模决定。

Method: 提出RCDH（可拒绝且标定的决策头），一种有类型、可拒绝、经标定的输出接口，可挂载于冻结的VLA骨干，无需重训练或新特征。包含：(i) 带显式拒绝与有序条件分解的决策schema；(ii) 面向风险感知弃权的标定流程。在具有可控叶片遮挡的机器人采摘平台上，对比生成式、枚举式、标定式和可拒绝式接口。

Result: 仅替换输出头即可在遮挡下恢复分布外（OOD）可用性，同时保持分布内性能；并进一步验证了拒绝空间排序的重要性。

Conclusion: 在不确定性下的可靠操作中，缺失的是"拒绝的权利"这一接口，而非更大的模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained with behavior cloning or flow matching are optimized to output an action trajectory, but they cannot express "I don't know" or "I should not act." In robotic harvesting, occlusion makes single-frame decisions fundamentally ambiguous: identical pixels can correspond either to a cuttable stem or to no stem at all. Existing VLAs are forced to commit, leading to high-confidence errors with irreversible consequences. We argue that the failure mode of a VLA is determined not by backbone scale but by its output interface. We propose Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads (RCDH), a typed, rejectable, and calibrated output interface that can be attached to a frozen VLA backbone without retraining or new features. RCDH introduces (i) a decision schema with explicit rejection and ordered, conditional decomposition, and (ii) a calibration procedure for risk-aware abstention. We evaluate RCDH on a robotic harvesting platform with controllable leaf occlusion, comparing generative, enumerated, calibrated, and rejectable interfaces. We show that replacing only the output head restores out-of-distribution usability under occlusion while preserving in-distribution performance. We further test whether the ordering of the rejection space is critical. Our results suggest that the right to refuse, rather than a larger model, is the missing interface for reliable manipulation under uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [EMPIRIC: Experiment-Driven Learning of Residual World Models for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35047)
*Yichao Liang,Amber Li,Dat Nguyen,Emily Bunnapradist,Michelangelo Naim,Sreela Kodali,Matteo Merler,Bowen Li,Kiran Gopinathan,Yiyun Liu,Nikhil Pimpalkhare,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Adrian Weller,Zenna Tavares,Tom Silver,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMPIRIC提出一种残差世界模型学习方法，将物理引擎与学习到的缺失机制代码结合，使机器人能在少量交互下学习、预测并规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理引擎虽能提供运动与接触知识，但常遗漏如胶水固化、水加热或风等完整机制，导致机器人难以准确预测陌生对象的交互行为。

Method: 将物理引擎扩展为残差世界模型，加入描述缺失机制的代码；学习程序可引入新力、约束与隐藏状态，并通过贝叶斯推断从噪声观测中估计其参数和状态；模型支持预测动作结果、选择信息量高的实验，并在预测失败时修正假设。

Result: 在五个模拟领域中，EMPIRIC学习到可解释、可复用的模型，并以更少的环境交互解决更多任务，优于三个基线；在真实机器人上学习了风力与多米诺骨牌质量，完成操作任务。

Conclusion: 通过将物理知识与可学习机制代码结合，EMPIRIC实现了高效、可解释且可迁移的机器人世界建模与任务规划。

Abstract: A robot should be able to learn through experiments how unfamiliar objects behave and interact, then plan with that knowledge. It need not start from scratch: physics engines supply knowledge of motion and contact, but can omit entire mechanisms, such as glue curing, water heating, or wind. We present EMPIRIC, an agent that learns a residual world model: a physics engine extended with code for the missing mechanisms. The learned programs can introduce new forces, constraints, and hidden state, and Bayesian inference estimates their parameters and states from noisy observations. The resulting model lets the agent predict the outcomes of actions, choose informative experiments, and revise its hypotheses when predictions fail. Across five simulated domains, EMPIRIC learns interpretable, reusable models, and solves more tasks with fewer environment interactions than all three baselines. On a physical robot, it learns wind forces and domino masses to solve a manipulation task. Website and code: https://yichao-liang.github.io/empiric

</details>


### [151] [QuadHand: A Compact Quadrotor Aerial Manipulator with MRC-SDF-Based Whole-Body Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35094)
*Rui Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Yi Wang,Yizhuo Yang,Lihua Xi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QuadHand——一种紧凑型四旋翼空中机械臂，配备3自由度机械臂、夹爪和电池辅助被动重心补偿模块；并提出MRC-SDF精细几何表征与时空全身轨迹优化框架，实现复杂环境中的安全可执行空中操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人物空中机械臂（UAM）将机械臂与空中平台结合，实现三维物理交互，但扩大工作空间会加剧机械臂引起的扰动；同时现有几何表征在近距离交互中难以兼顾几何保真度与计算效率。

Method: 设计紧凑型四旋翼空中机械臂QuadHand，含3自由度机械臂、夹爪及电池辅助被动重心补偿模块以抑制主要臂致扰动；提出MRC-SDF（多关节机器人中心符号距离场）在可控计算量下保留精细几何细节；并构建时空全身轨迹优化框架，联合优化四旋翼与机械臂。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，该方法可在复杂环境中实现安全且可执行的空中操作。

Conclusion: 结合被动重心补偿的紧凑硬件设计与高效精细的MRC-SDF表征及全身轨迹优化，可有效提升复杂环境下空中操作的安全性与可执行性。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial manipulators (UAMs) integrate robotic arms with aerial platforms for three-dimensional physical interaction. However, enlarging the workspace increases arm-induced disturbances, while existing geometric representations face a trade-off between geometric fidelity and computational efficiency in close-proximity interaction. This paper presents QuadHand, a compact quadrotor aerial manipulator with a 3-DoF arm, gripper, and battery-assisted passive CoG compensation module to reduce dominant arm-induced disturbances. We further propose MRC-SDF, a Multi-articulated Robot-Centric Signed Distance Field that preserves fine geometric detail with tractable computation, and a spatiotemporal whole-body trajectory optimization framework that jointly optimizes the quadrotor and manipulator for safe and executable trajectory generation. Simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate safe and executable aerial manipulation in complex environments.

</details>


### [152] [ReCAT: Remember, Count, and Time: Structured Recurrent Memory for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35200)
*Pankhuri Vanjani,Mostafa Hatab,Can Mizrakli,Vaisakh Shaj,Zhuoyue Li,Moritz Reuss,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReCAT,一种具备结构化循环记忆的语言条件策略,用于解决机器人记忆依赖型操作任务,在 LIBERO、RMBench 及真实机器人任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖型操作要求机器人利用当前传感器已无法获取的信息(如回忆早期视觉线索、跟踪任务进度、计数重复事件、估计经过时间)进行决策,现有策略缺乏有效的结构化记忆机制。

Method: 指令条件编码器从当前观测提取特征;循环记忆通过 Mamba-2 层与一个因果注意力层整合观测流;流匹配 Transformer 解码器在每个模块中通过独立交叉注意力分别读取当前与历史表示。并在 ReCAT 内部进行受控对比,考察观测编码器、逐块记忆条件以及不同更新规则(加法更新、delta-rule 更新)的影响。

Result: LIBERO 平均成功率 95.3%,RMBench 62.4%,在 9 项任务中 6 项达到最佳或并列最佳;三项真实机器人任务(空间回忆、事件计数、间隔计时)中最佳 ReCAT 变体平均成功率 66.7%,最强短历史基线仅 8.3%。受控实验表明观测编码器与逐块记忆条件不可或缺;加法更新在计数与计时上表现最好,delta-rule 更新在空间回忆上最好。

Conclusion: ReCAT 通过结构化循环记忆有效支撑记忆依赖型机器人操作,且不同记忆更新规则适配不同任务类型,为机器人记忆建模提供了设计依据。

Abstract: Memory-dependent manipulation requires robots to make decisions using information that is no longer available to their current sensors, such as recalling an earlier visual cue, tracking task progress, counting repeated events, or estimating elapsed time. We present ReCAT, a language-conditioned policy with structured recurrent memory. An instruction-conditioned encoder forms features from the current observation. A recurrent memory integrates the observation stream through Mamba-2 layers and one causal attention layer. A flow-matching Transformer decoder reads the current and the historical representation through separate cross-attention in every block. ReCAT reaches 95.3\% average success on LIBERO and 62.4\% on RMBench, with the best or tied-best result on six of nine tasks. On three real-robot tasks probing spatial recall, event counting, and interval timing, the best ReCAT variant reaches 66.7\% average success, against 8.3\% for the strongest short-history baseline. Controlled comparisons within ReCAT show that the observation encoder and every-block memory conditioning are needed for this performance. They also show that update rules developed for efficient sequence modeling behave differently as robot memory: additive updates have the highest observed success on counting and timing, and delta-rule updates on spatial recall. Project website is at https://intuitive-robots.github.io/ReCAT

</details>


### [153] [Zero-Shot Reactive Obstacle Avoidance for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35231)
*Weihang Guo,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的机器人避障方法 NUDGE，通过在推理时将符号距离场梯度注入扩散/流匹配策略，实现实时反应式避障，且不改变原策略的任务分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略与视觉-语言-动作模型等机器人策略在任务执行上表现优异，但缺乏对障碍物的主动规避能力。现有避障方案往往需要重新训练策略，成本高且可能破坏原有任务性能，因此需要一种通用、免训练、可即插即用的避障手段。

Method: NUDGE 在推理阶段，从符号距离场（SDF，返回每个点到最近障碍物的距离）中计算梯度，并将其注入到基于扩散或流匹配的策略中，从而引导动作远离障碍物。通过可微分的关节轨迹解码器，方法支持多种常见动作参数化形式，包括绝对/相对关节位姿以及末端执行器位姿。

Result: 实验表明，NUDGE 能够在保留策略原有任务分布的同时，以反应式方式实时运行。

Conclusion: NUDGE 是一种通用、免训练、实时的机器人避障方案，可无缝集成到扩散策略和视觉-语言-动作模型中，适用于多种动作参数化形式。

Abstract: We propose NUDGE (Nudge Update via Differentiable GEometry), a training-free obstacle-avoidance procedure that can be incorporated in any robot policy based on diffusion or flow matching, including diffusion policies and vision-language-action models. Our work injects gradients from a signed distance field, a function returning each point's distance to the nearest obstacle, into the policy at inference time to steer it away from obstacles. It supports any common action parameterization, from absolute or relative joint poses to end-effector poses, through a differentiable joint-trajectory decoder. Experiments show that NUDGE preserves the policy's task distribution and runs reactively in real time.

</details>


### [154] [Spatial Grafting: Grounding 3D Features for Flow-Matching Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35249)
*Dingsheng Liu,Yangzheng Wu,Mahboubeh Asadi,Zhiyuan Li,Jinbang Huang,Yixin Xiao,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Spatial Grafting，一种轻量空间模块，将冻结的稀疏重建特征绑定到机器人相对度量几何，并通过交叉注意力注入流匹配动作专家，提升多种预训练策略在仿真与真机任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的机器人操作策略（如 VLA、WAM）隐式地处理交互相关的度量几何，而空间重建特征虽能提供可靠几何，却只描述局部形状、不指明其相对机器人的位置；如何将这些特征有效传递给预训练策略仍无定论。

Method: Spatial Grafting 不修改宿主策略的感知通路，而是构造具有度量、机器人相对几何基础的 spatial tokens，并经由 cross-attention 注入到 flow-matching 动作专家中；同一架构无需针对宿主重新设计即可适配多种 VLA 与 WAM。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上，该模块提升所有宿主模型；Grafted π0.5 在干净与随机场景分别提升 11.3% 和 15.6%，达 94.0% 与 92.4%，超过最强已发布 3D 条件策略 WAM4D（93.8%、89.9%）。在 BEHAVIOR-1K 上，六个任务中五个超过 2025 挑战赛冠军，最高提升 0.47 Q-score，并平均超过地图条件空间策略。

Conclusion: 统一的 spatial grafting 架构可跨两个 VLA、两个 WAM、四个仿真基准与三个真机平台泛化，且随任务时域变长优势更明显，说明将度量几何以机器人相对方式注入动作专家是提升预训练策略空间感知的有效途径。

Abstract: Pretrained robot manipulation policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) or world-action models (WAMs) leave interaction-relevant metric geometry implicit. Recent breakthroughs in spatial reconstruction can supply the necessary geometry reliably, but their features describe local shape without stating where it lies with respect to the robot. How best to deliver these features to a pretrained policy remains unresolved. We propose Spatial Grafting, a versatile, lightweight spatial module that binds frozen reconstruction features to metric, robot-relative geometry. Spatial Grafting constructs metric-grounded spatial tokens and injects them into the flow-matching action expert through cross-attention, without modifying the host's perceptual pathway, so the host retains the full benefit of its pretraining. We evaluate it more broadly than any geometry-aware policy we compare against: one graft architecture, with no per-host redesign, on two VLAs and two WAMs, across four simulation benchmarks that span short-horizon manipulation, visual robustness, clutter and long-horizon mobile manipulation, and on three real-robot platforms with single- and dual-arm configurations. On RoboTwin 2.0, a dual-arm manipulation benchmark, the graft improves every host across VLAs and WAMs. Grafted $π_{0.5}$ gains 11.3% and 15.6% on clean and randomized scenes, reaching 94.0% and 92.4%, above the strongest published 3D-conditioned policy, WAM4D (93.8% and 89.9%). The margin widens as the horizon lengthens: on tasks from BEHAVIOR-1K, a dual-arm mobile manipulation challenge scored by average task progress, it surpasses the 2025 challenge winner on five of six tasks,by up to 0.47 Q-score, and exceeds a map-conditioned spatial policy on average across the three tasks both report.

</details>


### [155] [Repair Before You Fuse: Frozen-Host Adaptation for Corrupted-but-Present Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35264)
*Gia-Huy Thai,Quang-Thinh Ly,Anh-Minh Phan,Tuan Dang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出边界特征修复（BFR）框架，在不重训已部署检测器的前提下，于模态接口学习任务监督的残差修正，提升相机-LiDAR检测器在损坏数据下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-LiDAR检测器即使在两传感器均存在、同步且已标定的情况下，仍会持续消费不可靠特征。现有方法通常需要干净参考、损坏元数据、时序历史或在线更新，部署代价高。

Method: BFR 是一种冻结主机的适配框架，仅在检测器已消费的模态接口处学习任务监督的残差修正。BFR-C 修复融合读取的每个相机特征层级；BFR-L 将主机条件化的 LiDAR 候选对齐到选定边界，并相对冻结锚点路由站点级新息；二者联合训练组成 BFR-CL。逐通道缩放零初始化使各变体在优化前是精确的检测器级恒等映射，仅训练修复模块，编码器、融合消费者、路由器、检测头与主机归一化统计量均固定。推理时无需干净参考、损坏元数据、时序历史或在线更新。

Result: 在完整 20 种损坏、5 个严重度的 KITTI-C 网格上，BFR-C 将 MVX-Net 的 RCE 从 14.07 降至 11.92，将 Focals Conv-F 的 RCE 从 14.29 降至 11.00。在后者主机上，BFR-L 将 AP_cor 从 73.65 提升至 74.48，BFR-CL 达到 77.01 AP_cor 与 10.46 RCE，干净 AP 为 86.02。在 nuScenes-R 上，BFR-CL 将复现的 MoME 基线的 mAP 鲁棒性比从 80.1 提升至 81.4。

Conclusion: 结果确立了边界修复作为面向"损坏但存在"传感场景的定向改造方案，无需重训已部署检测器即可提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR detectors can continue to consume unreliable features even when both sensors remain present, synchronized, and calibrated. We introduce \emph{Boundary Feature Repair} (BFR), a frozen-host adaptation framework that learns task-supervised residual corrections at modality interfaces the detector already consumes. BFR-C repairs each camera feature level read by fusion, whereas BFR-L aligns host-conditioned LiDAR candidates to a selected boundary and routes site-wise innovations relative to the frozen anchor. Their jointly trained composition is BFR-CL. Zero-initialized per-channel scales make every variant an exact detector-level identity before optimization; only the repair modules train, while the encoders, fusion consumer, router, detection head, and host normalization statistics remain fixed. At inference, BFR requires neither clean references, corruption metadata, temporal history, nor online updates. Across the complete 20-corruption, five-severity KITTI-C grid, BFR-C reduces RCE from $14.07$ to $11.92$ on MVX-Net and from $14.29$ to $11.00$ on Focals Conv-F relative to their reproduced frozen baselines. On the latter host, BFR-L raises AP$_{\mathrm{cor}}$ from $73.65$ to $74.48$, while BFR-CL reaches $77.01$ AP$_{\mathrm{cor}}$ and $10.46$ RCE with $86.02$ clean AP. On nuScenes-R, BFR-CL raises the reproduced MoME baseline's mAP robustness ratio from $80.1$ to $81.4$. These results establish boundary repair as a targeted retrofit for corrupted-but-present sensing without retraining the deployed detector.

</details>


### [156] [GuardPIBT: Counterfactually Gated Neural Guidance for Ultra-Large-Scale 3D Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35267)
*Yuan Zhou,Zhenyu Hou,Guangtong Xu,Xiaoqiang Ji,Yuqing Tang,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GuardPIBT，在不替换PIBT执行器前提下，用神经预测仅对候选重排序提供残差调整，实现超大规�模3D多智能体路径规划，最高10万智能体可靠完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模3D多智能体路径规划在密集交通下难度激增。PIBT可扩展性好，但其单步目标导向排序在密集交互与大规模拥堵下不足。

Method: 保留PIBT执行器（有效性检查、优先级继承、回溯），神经预测仅提出候选的残差重排序。包括：局部图注意力建模邻近交互，全局源-目标运输特征提供群体级协调上下文；反事实组门控过滤可能降低协调的重排序；超大规模下采用种群自适应分组保持决策粒度、异步缓存推理摊薄神经计算、选择性修复处理长尾智能体。

Result: 在最多100,000智能体的实验中，于2D与3D环境实现可靠完成，包括全部三次10万智能体仓库运行，审计图违规为零。

Conclusion: 通过将神经预测安全地限制为PIBT候选的残差重排序并保留PIBT核心机制，可在密集拥堵与超大规模场景下保持有效性与可扩展性，兼顾协调质量与安全性。

Abstract: Large-scale 3D multi-agent path finding becomes increasingly difficult under dense traffic. Priority Inheritance with Backtracking (PIBT) scales well, but its one-step goal-directed ordering may become insufficient under dense interactions and large-scale congestion. We present GuardPIBT, which augments rather than replaces the PIBT executor: neural predictions only propose residual reorderings of PIBT's native candidates, while final actions remain determined by PIBT. First, local graph attention models nearby interactions, while global source--goal transport features provide population-level coordination context for candidate reordering. Second, a counterfactual group gate filters reorderings whose closed-loop effects may degrade coordination. Third, for ultra-large populations, population-adaptive grouping preserves decision granularity, asynchronous cached inference amortizes neural computation, and selective repair resolves long-tail agents. PIBT retains validity checking, priority inheritance, and backtracking throughout. Experiments with up to 100,000 agents demonstrate reliable completion across 2D and 3D environments, including all three 100,000-agent warehouse runs with zero audited graph violations. The project website is available at {\color{magenta}\texttt{https://guardpibt.github.io/GuardPIBT/}}.

</details>


### [157] [DexAgent: An Agentic Human2Sim2Robot Framework for Dexterous Manipulation with Self-Evolving Tool Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35318)
*Youhui Wang,Yunzhu Li,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Huang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexAgent是一个智能体式Human2Sim2Robot框架，将单段第一人称人类视频与任务提示转化为物理可信的机器人轨迹，用于策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是可扩展的灵巧操作演示来源，但现有Human2Sim2Robot流程依赖预定义程序，难以适应多样化物体属性与交互，尤其是铰接物体和可变形物体。

Method: 框架含四阶段：人类视频语义理解、基于属性的仿真重建、机器人轨迹优化、机器人数据生成。每阶段DexAgent从工具库选择或新建技能，由属性特定的验证器评估结果并提供反馈以迭代精炼，防止误差传播；新技能与验证器存入工具库实现自进化。

Result: 在11项真实世界任务中，使用DexAgent生成数据训练的策略成功率比竞争基线高3.5倍。

Conclusion: DexAgent通过自适应、验证引导的流程处理多样物体与长时序任务，并随经验积累提升效率，实现自进化与可复用能力积累。

Abstract: Human videos offer a scalable source of demonstrations for dexterous robot manipulation. However, existing human-to-simulation-to-robot (Human2Sim2Robot) pipelines rely on predefined procedures that struggle to accommodate diverse object properties and interactions, particularly those involving articulated and deformable objects. We introduce DexAgent, an agentic Human2Sim2Robot framework that converts a single egocentric human video and a task prompt into physically grounded robot trajectories for policy training. It operates through four stages: semantic understanding of human videos, property-based simulation reconstruction, robot trajectory optimization, and robot data generation. At each stage, DexAgent adapts its approach to the task and object properties by selecting suitable skills from its tool library or developing new ones when needed. Property-specific verifiers assess stage outcomes for physical validity and task-specific requirements and provide feedback for refinement, preventing error propagation through the workflow. This adaptive, verification-guided process allows DexAgent to process diverse objects and long-horizon tasks. In the final stage, DexAgent varies object and robot states in simulation to generate diverse robot trajectories from a single human video, then retextures the rendered observations to facilitate sim-to-real transfer. Newly developed skills and verifiers are retained in its tool library, making it self-evolving to accumulate reusable capabilities. This reduces processing time as DexAgent encounters more human videos. Across eleven real-world tasks, policies trained with DexAgent-generated data achieve a 3.5x higher success rate than competing baselines. Project website: https://dexagent123.github.io/.

</details>


### [158] [From Pixel to Poses: Object-centric Tool Manipulation Learning from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35375)
*Bangjun Wang,Longyan Wu,Yukun Wei,Shenghe Shao,Chaoyi Huang,Wenze Cui,Zetong Xu,Hanlin Wu,Long Chen,Yi Ma,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P2P-T是一个数据高效、以物体为中心的框架，能直接从人类演示中学习工具使用，无需人-机器人对齐数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展机器人操作的主要瓶颈是真实机器人数据稀缺。现有利用人类视频演示的方法计算开销大，且仍需配对的人-机器人数据做域对齐；同时现有方法擅长长时程任务，却在复杂工具操作所需的精细控制上表现不佳。

Method: P2P-T采用两阶段方法：先预训练一个以物体为中心的世界模型以提取稳定的位姿先验；再将这些先验整合进一个高效的、位姿感知的底层策略。并借助由现代基础模型驱动的自动数据处理流程，完全绕过人-机器人对齐数据的需求。

Result: 在复杂真实世界工具操作任务上，以极少的单任务微调，执行性能较此前最先进方法提升73%，而这类任务标准大规模预训练模型目前仍无法完成。

Conclusion: P2P-T通过从像素到位姿的、以物体为中心的两阶段设计，桥接了认知与物理执行之间的差距，大幅降低训练开销，并在高精度工具操作任务上显著超越现有方法。

Abstract: Scaling up robotic manipulation is primarily bottlenecked by the scarcity of real-world robot data. While recent approaches leverage human video demonstrations to mitigate this shortage, they remain computationally expensive and still rely on paired human-robot data for domain alignment. Although current state-of-the-arts excel at long-horizon tasks, they struggle with the delicate and precise control required for complex tool manipulation. To overcome these limitations, we introduce P2P-T, from Pixel to Poses for Tool Manipulation, a data-efficient, object-centric framework that learns tool use directly from human demonstrations. P2P-T bridges the cognitive and physical execution gap through a two-stage approach. First, pretraining an object-centric world model to extract stable pose priors; second, integrating these priors into an efficient, pose-aware low-level policy. By utilizing a robust automated data processing pipeline powered by modern foundation models, P2P-T completely bypasses the need for human-robot aligned data. This reduces overall training overhead drastically. With minimal per-task fine-tuning, our framework achieves a 73% improvement over the previous state of the art in execution performance on complex, real-world tool manipulation tasks that currently remain out of reach for standard large-scale pretrained models.

</details>


### [159] [Revision, Not Restart: Revisable Visual Plans for Closed-Loop World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35439)
*Pengyiang Liu,Junbo Niu,Wenhao Zheng,Xinchen Chen,Canyu Li,Zhongyue Shi,Jiahao Xie,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可修订时序规划（RTP），将视觉未来作为持续动作条件并在执行反馈后加以修订，在RoboMME与RMBench上取得48.6%和84.8%的任务平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型依赖预测的视觉未来来条件化机器人动作，但执行反馈可能使预测的部分内容失效，而其任务结构仍然有用；因此需要在保留有用结构的同时对预测进行修订。

Method: RTP维护视觉未来作为持续的动作条件，并在反馈后修订。核心机制是学习式修订桥：恢复视觉生成过程中保存的中间状态，并根据当前观测调整其延续。视觉与动作监督将该修订连接到后续控制；时间感知历史提供观测证据；自适应策略在新噪声基础上选择保留、桥接修订或全新重规划，然后解码下一步动作。

Result: 在RoboMME和RMBench上，RTP分别达到48.6%和84.8%的任务平均成功率。匹配对比支持学习式延续；估计的检查点来源效应与动作前缀效应为正，但精度较低。

Conclusion: 结果将反馈驱动的视觉规划修订与闭环任务性能联系起来，说明保留并修订视觉未来对机器人动作控制具有实际价值。

Abstract: World-action models use predicted visual futures to condition robot actions, yet execution feedback can invalidate parts of a prediction while leaving its task structure useful. We propose Revisable Temporal Planning (RTP), which maintains the visual future as a persistent action condition and revises it after feedback. Its central mechanism is a learned revision bridge: it resumes an intermediate state saved during visual generation and adapts its continuation to current observations. Visual and action supervision connect this revision to subsequent control. Time-aware history supplies observed evidence, and an adaptive policy selects retention, bridge revision, or fresh replanning from new noise before decoding the next action. On RoboMME and RMBench, RTP achieves task-averaged success rates of 48.6% and 84.8%, respectively. Matched comparisons support learned continuation; estimated checkpoint-source and action-prefix effects are positive but less precisely resolved. These results connect feedback-driven visual-plan revision to closed-loop task performance. Project Page: https://PLACEHOLDER.github.io/RTP/

</details>


### [160] [Uni-VLaT: Whole-Body Tactile Adaptation of VLA Policies for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35450)
*Zihao Wang,Shutong Liu,Siqi Zheng,Liu Cao,Ruoqi Chen,Rundong Liu,Yanchao Yang,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-VLaT将全身分布式触觉信息集成到VLA策略中，通过预测未来多模态表示实现接触丰富控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠视觉和本体感知难以刻画物理交互，尤其接触区域被遮挡时；稀疏力/力矩测量不足以捕捉空间接触模式。

Method: 引入触觉通路，其潜在状态不仅用于动作生成，还预测未来触觉、本体感知和视觉表示，构建以触觉锚定的多模态上下文。

Result: 在五项真实机器人任务上平均成功率75%，比无视触觉基线高43分，比无预测监督的触觉基线高7分；在两个VLA骨干上Table Sweeping提升30分，Back-Tap Walking提升85-90分。

Conclusion: 预测性触觉学习为扩展预训练VLA策略至全身物理交互提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Physical contact often determines how a humanoid should respond during loco-manipulation, yet vision and proprioception alone are often insufficient to characterize physical interaction, especially when the contact region is occluded. Unlike sparse force or torque measurements at predefined regions, distributed tactile sensing preserves spatially resolved contact patterns across the robot body. We therefore study how to integrate such whole-body tactile information into vision-language-action (VLA) policies for contact-rich control. Our approach, Uni-VLaT, introduces a tactile pathway whose latent state is trained not only for action generation, but also to predict future tactile, proprioceptive, and visual representations. This predictive objective builds a tactile-anchored multimodal context, encouraging a more structured understanding of the physical world. We evaluate Uni-VLaT on five real-robot tasks covering tactile-triggered locomotion, sustained physical interaction, human-robot contact, and loco-manipulation. Uni-VLaT achieves a 75% average success rate, outperforming a baseline without tactile input by 43 points and a tactile-input baseline without predictive supervision by 7 points. Across two pretrained VLA backbones, our method improves Table Sweeping by 30 points on both backbones and Back-Tap Walking by 85-90 points. Ablations further show that contextualized tactile prediction and absolute future targets are critical to performance. These results indicate that predictive tactile learning provides an effective route for extending pretrained VLA policies to whole-body physical interaction.

</details>


### [161] [Denoising Multi-Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35651)
*Yuhao Zhang,Keisuke Okumura,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文基于D4orm扩散去噪框架，提出一套多机器人轨迹规划架构，在仿真与真实平台上实现快速、可靠、可扩展的协调规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人轨迹规划本质上是非凸、多模态、高维的优化问题，计算难度大。传统数值优化方法存在局限，需要一种更高效、通用的规划范式。

Method: 以D4orm（动力学感知的扩散去噪框架）为基础，采用基于采样的优化，通过GPU大规模并行采样生成轨迹；其扩散去噪结构迭代优化候选控制轨迹的"形变"，从而生成动力学可行且无冲突的轨迹。在此基础上构建三种规划器：解耦式规划器（提升可扩展性）、带反馈控制的在线滚动时域规划器、面向资源受限场景的分布式规划器。

Result: 在2D/3D环境下针对差速驱动机器人和全向机器人评估，D4orm类方法相比MPPI等采样优化方法及基于学习扩散模型的方法，更快更可靠地找到高质量解；并实现十架真实四旋翼避障零样本部署、100个仿真机器人大规模解冲突、六台地面机器人全机载分布式"终身"运行。

Conclusion: 研究确立了扩散去噪作为多机器人协调的可扩展且可靠的框架。

Abstract: Multi-robot trajectory planning is a fundamental problem in multi-robot coordination but remains computationally challenging due to its nonconvex, multimodal, and high-dimensional nature. This work builds upon D4orm, a dynamics-aware diffusion-denoising framework, and develops a family of planning architectures for diverse operational requirements. Unlike conventional numerical optimization methods, D4orm employs sampling-based optimization to generate solution trajectories through massively parallel sampling, leveraging modern computing architectures such as GPUs. Its diffusion-denoising structure iteratively optimizes \textit{deformations} to candidate control trajectories, providing an efficient and versatile paradigm for generating kinodynamically feasible and conflict-free trajectories. Using D4orm as the building block for advanced planners, we present a decoupled planner for improved scalability, an online receding-horizon planner with feedback control, and a distributed planner for resource-constrained settings. Evaluations with differential-drive and holonomic robots in 2D and 3D environments demonstrate that D4orm-based approaches find high-quality solutions faster and more reliably than other sampling-based optimization methods, such as MPPI, as well as a learned diffusion-model-based method. We further demonstrate zero-shot deployment on ten real quadrotors with obstacles, large-scale deconfliction with 100 simulated robots, and fully onboard distributed `lifelong' operation with six ground robots. Overall, these results establish diffusion denoising as a scalable and reliable framework for multi-robot coordination. Code and video: https://github.com/proroklab/d4orm

</details>


### [162] [MM-ABC: Towards Generalist Mobile Manipulation via Seeing, Coordinating and Imagining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35652)
*Qiwei Liang,Guangyu Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Zikuan Xiao,Jinxuan Lu,Yifan Xie,Renjing Xu,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MM-ABC移动操作基础模型，通过"看见-协调-想象"实现臂基跨流协作，在多个基准和真实任务上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作使可达区域本身可控，带来两大挑战：连续自我运动下的空间感知，以及臂与基座异构动作的协调控制。现有方法或用显式3D表示/预测式世界模型强化几何，或将移动与操作解耦为独立动作流。作者认为有效移动操作不仅需要解耦，还需支持跨流高效协作的表示。

Method: MM-ABC围绕Seeing、Coordinating、Imagining的臂基协作构建：采用稀疏多层级VLM特征做空间感知；引入仅在训练时使用的未来分支，以世界想象和几何意图作为额外监督；提出MM-APT，通过掩码联合注意力和clean-action x-prediction协调操作与移动两条独立动作流。

Result: 消融显示：将clean-action预测替换为速度预测，RoboCasa365 composite-seen成功率从32.8%降至29.2%；移除未来监督或多层级条件则下降更大。在5000+小时、400K+回合、12个数据集、17种本体上预训练。EBench 44.71%，RoboCasa365 61.2%，LIBERO 99.1%，LIBERO-Plus（无扰动训练）82.8%，五项真实任务平均成功率83%。

Conclusion: 跨流协作表示与训练期未来监督对移动操作至关重要；解耦动作流配合掩码联合注意力与clean-action预测能显著提升多基准及真实场景表现。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation extends robot interaction beyond a fixed kinematic workspace by making the reachable region itself controllable. This flexibility introduces two central challenges: spatially grounded perception under continuous ego-motion and coordinated control of heterogeneous arm and base actions. Existing approaches strengthen geometry through explicit 3D representations or predictive world models, and often decouple mobility and manipulation into separate action streams. We argue that effective mobile manipulation requires not only decoupling, but also representations that support efficient cross-stream collaboration. We present MM-ABC, a foundation model built around Seeing, Coordinating, and Imagining Arm-Base Collaboration. MM-ABC combines sparse multi-level VLM features for spatial perception; a training-only future branch that uses world imagination and geometric intent as extra supervision, strengthening perception and manipulation-intent prediction and improving the overall learning signal; and MM-APT, which coordinates separate manipulation and mobility streams through masked joint attention and clean-action x-prediction. In controlled ablations, replacing clean-action prediction with velocity prediction lowers success on RoboCasa365 composite-seen tasks from 32.8% to 29.2%, and removing future supervision or multilevel conditioning causes larger drops. We pretrain MM-ABC on 5,000+ hours of heterogeneous robot data spanning 400K+ episodes, 12 datasets, and 17 embodiments. Experiments cover EBench, RoboCasa365, ManiSkill-HAB, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world mobile manipulation. MM-ABC achieves 44.71% success on EBench, 61.2% on RoboCasa365, 99.1% on LIBERO, 82.8% on LIBERO-Plus without perturbation training, and 83% mean success on five real-world tasks.

</details>


### [163] [Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35690)
*Puming Jiang,Tianrun Hu,Haozhe Du,Yibo Li,Zhiwei Xue,Xinhu Li,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用策略的结构先验作为技能组合与技能学习之间的接口，并实例化为APPL框架，在MetaWorld和ManiSkill任务上提升了分布外技能泛化与未见技能组合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量示范中学习的机器人需要两种泛化：组合泛化（重组技能解决新任务）与技能泛化（策略在新情境下有效）。二者相互依赖，但组合与技能之间的信息传递存在损失：技能适用场景由其策略训练时的结构决定，而组合仅通过名称、指令或符号算子等描述看到技能，丢失了结构信息。

Method: 提出以每个策略的结构先验作为组合与技能之间接口的核心思想。结构先验陈述行为依赖什么（如抓取仅依赖夹爪相对物体的位姿）。训练时内置该先验以塑造策略泛化范围，用语言表述以告知组合该策略的适用范围。实例化为Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning（APPL）：构建智能体将完整示范分割为可复用技能，为每个技能提出多个结构先验，并为每个先验训练和验证一个策略；运行时智能体在这些先验特定策略间选择，并利用其接口组合成新任务目标。

Result: 在MetaWorld和长时程ManiSkill任务上，APPL提升了分布外技能泛化能力，并实现了此前未见的技能组合；消融接口信息会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将训练时的结构假设作为技能学习与技能组合之间的桥梁是有效的。

Abstract: Robots that learn from a few demonstrations often require two forms of generalization. Compositional generalization recombines skills to solve new tasks, and skill generalization lets the learned policy behind each skill work in new situations. The two depend on each other, yet information is lost between composition and the skills it calls. Where a skill works is determined by the structure its policy is trained with, while composition sees the skill only through a separate description, such as a name, an instruction, or a symbolic operator, that omits this structure. Our key idea is to use each policy's structural prior as part of the interface between composition and the skill. A structural prior states what a behavior depends on, for example that a grasp depends only on the gripper's pose relative to the object. Built into training, it shapes where the policy generalizes; stated in language, it tells composition where the policy applies. We instantiate this idea in Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning (APPL). A construction agent segments complete demonstrations into reusable skills, proposes several structural priors for each skill, and trains and verifies one policy per prior. A runtime agent then selects among these prior-specific policies and composes them toward new task goals using their interfaces. Across MetaWorld and long-horizon ManiSkill tasks, APPL improves out-of-distribution skill generalization and enables previously unseen skill compositions; ablating the interface information substantially reduces performance. These results support the use of training-time structural assumptions as a bridge between skill learning and skill composition.

</details>


### [164] [LQR-ArUco Fusion: Robust Hierarchical Control for Navigation and Asymmetric Manipulation in Two-Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35700)
*Anupam Chatterjee,Arpita Kumari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层控制框架，用于解决两轮倒立摆（TWIP）机器人在进行非对称物体操作时出现的严重动态不稳定和导航漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两轮平台机动性强，但持续平衡调整使车载里程计极不可靠；同时侧挂机械臂举起负载时会引入无驱动的横向滚转力矩，在不平地面上问题更为严重。

Method: 采用分层架构分工：离板视觉系统通过顶置ArUco标记提供高延迟全局航点导航以绕过里程计漂移；同时低延迟板载控制回路利用惯性单元和编码器数据主动抑制物理扰动。

Result: 在物理实验中，该双回路方法使自制机器人能够精确导航、抵御减速带的瞬态冲击、适应动态跷跷板坡道，并在窄轮距下安全携带负载而不倾倒。

Conclusion: 该双回路分层控制框架有效解决了TWIP机器人非对称操作中的耦合稳定性与导航问题，并在多项物理实验中验证了其鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose a hierarchical control framework to address severe dynamic instabilities and navigational drift that arise when a two-wheeled inverted pendulum (TWIP) robot attempts asymmetric object manipulation. While two-wheeled platforms are highly manoeuvrable, their constant balancing adjustments make onboard odometry highly unreliable for precise navigation. Furthermore, the addition of a side-mounted robotic arm introduces unactuated lateral roll moments when a payload is lifted, a challenge heavily compounded on uneven terrain. To solve these coupled problems, our architecture divides the workload. An offboard vision system tracks overhead ArUco markers to provide high-latency global waypoint navigation, bypassing odometry drift. Simultaneously, a low-latency onboard control loop rejects active physical disturbances using inertial and encoder data. In our physical experiments, this dual-loop approach enabled the custom-built robot to navigate accurately, reject transient impacts from speed bumps, adapt to a dynamic seesaw ramp, and carry a payload securely without falling over its narrow wheelbase.

</details>


### [165] [Humanoid Loco-Manipulation With Discrete VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35709)
*Wenxin Shao,Siqi Chai,Kun Li,Kerou Zhang,Xinzhou Jiang,Wei Xu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Holo-M 是首个面向人形机器人全身 loco-manipulation 的离散 VLA 模型，通过将动作 token 扩展进语言模型词表、统一动作分词与分组离散扩散解码，在 SIMPLE 基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人全身动作空间（腿、躯干、手臂、手）维度高且异构，带来分词、训练与实时推理挑战；以往面向机械臂的离散动作 token VLA 模型无法应对这些问题。同时，使用独立连续动作专家的模型存在知识隔离（knowledge-insulation）问题。

Method: 提出 Holo-M：以离散 VLA 架构扩展语言模型词表加入动作 token。设计统一动作分词器，将人形动作空间分解为四个身体部位专用分词器——末端执行器、身体、手和运动学，从而支持跨不同本体与数据源（人形遥操作、第一人称人类视频、仿真）的训练。实时控制方面，采用分组离散扩散解码替代对动作 token 的自回归解码。

Result: 在 SIMPLE 人形 loco-manipulation 基准上进行了大量实验，Holo-M 在通用与专用评测中均取得最高成功率，且显著领先第二名。

Conclusion: Holo-M 有效解决人形全身动作的离散化与实时推理难题，验证了将动作 token 原生融入语言模型的可行性；作者将公开全部代码与模型权重。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models using discrete action tokens have proven effective for controling robotic arms on manipulation tasks. For a humanoid, however, the whole-body action space -- legs, torso, arms, and hands -- is far higher-dimensional and heterogeneous, raising tokenization, training, and real-time inference challenges that the previous VLA models do not address. We present Holo-M, to our knowledge the first discrete VLA model for humanoid loco-manipulation that intrinsically exploits the language model by extending its vocabulary with action tokens. In this model, we devise a unified action tokenizer that decomposes the humanoid action space into four body-part-specific tokenizers -- end-effector, body, hand, and kinematics -- enabling training across drastically different embodiments and data sources, including humanoid teleoperation, ego-centric human video, and simulation. By extending the language model's vocabulary with these action tokens, we avoid the knowledge-insulation problem inherent to the models that use separate continuous action experts. To meet real-time control requirements, we decode each body part's action tokens through grouped discrete diffusion decoding, rather than using autoregression on the action tokens. We have conducted extensive experiments on the SIMPLE humanoid loco-manipulation benchmark, in which Holo-M achieves the highest success rates in both the generalist and specialist evaluations, leading the second best by significant margins. We will release all the code and model weights.

</details>


### [166] [RoboCompiler: Graph-Native Compilation of Closed-Chain Robots for Consistent Modeling, Control, and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35717)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Joongheon Kim,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboCompiler——一种图原生框架，将规范机构图编译为共享机械接口，统一处理闭链、耦合驱动与变接触机器人中的构型、运动、力与动力学，并在多平台上验证了精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含运动学闭环、耦合执行器与变接触的机器人需要一致的构型、运动、力与动力学模型。但控制与仿真中这些接口常被分别重建，导致闭链约束与驱动一致性难以维持。

Method: 以机构图为核心：由刚体、关节、坐标系、惯量与执行器端口构建闭链路径及解析残差雅可比；通过秩检查的延拓与修正装配可行构型；用切空间提升将独立速度映射为全机器人与任务运动，配对执行器端口映射保持虚功；引入约束曲率修正，将降维扩展到加速度与投影刚体动力学（含浮动基与支撑模式）；支持周期局部求值、生成式雅可比与依赖感知复用，实现闭链输入变化时的局部更新。

Result: 在 Komatsu 挖掘机、Unitree Go2、Franka Panda、Kangaroo 与六-UPS Stewart 平台上评估物理闭环与任务诱导约束。高精度约束动力学及独立 Pinocchio 校验确认机械一致性；MuJoCo 与 Isaac Sim/PhysX 执行展示原生接触下的任务性能与模型复用。对 Kangaroo，编译使残差与雅可比求值时间减少 96.7%，闭环 rollout 墙钟时间减少 66.8%，且动力学与控制保持不变。

Conclusion: RoboCompiler 通过图原生共享接口统一了闭链与驱动一致性，兼顾精度与效率，可复用于控制与仿真，并在多种机器人系统上得到验证。

Abstract: Robots with kinematic loops, coupled actuators, and changing contacts require consistent models of configuration, motion, force, and dynamics. Yet these interfaces are often reconstructed separately for control and simulation, making closure and actuation consistency difficult to maintain. This paper presents RoboCompiler, a graph-native framework that compiles a canonical mechanism graph into a shared mechanical interface. From bodies, joints, frames, inertias, and actuator ports, it constructs closure paths and analytic residual Jacobians, then assembles feasible configurations through rank-checked continuation and correction. A tangent lift maps independent velocities to full robot and task motion, while paired actuator-port maps preserve virtual work. A constraint-curvature correction extends the reduction to accelerations and projected rigid-body dynamics, including floating-base and support modes. Cycle-local evaluation, generated Jacobians, and dependency-aware reuse enable localized updates when closure inputs change. We evaluate physical loops and task-induced constraints on a Komatsu excavator, Unitree Go2, Franka Panda, Kangaroo, and a six-UPS Stewart platform. High-precision constrained-dynamics and independent Pinocchio checks confirm mechanical consistency; MuJoCo and Isaac Sim/PhysX executions demonstrate task performance and model reuse under native contact. For Kangaroo, compilation reduces residual-and-Jacobian evaluation time by 96.7% and closed-loop rollout wall time by 66.8%, with dynamics and control held fixed.

</details>


### [167] [DexRoam: Learning Mobile Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Whole-Body Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35761)
*Rui Zhou,Yibo Yuan,Junkai Zhao,Fangyuan Zhao,Xiaoguang Zhao,Shanghang Zhang,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexRoam 提出一套从人类示教学习移动双臂灵巧操作的系统，全程保留连续耦合的全身运动，通过无追踪器采集与三阶段对齐，将人类示教用于 VLA 策略训练并显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动双臂灵巧操作需在单条轨迹中持续协调移动、全身运动与手指级灵巧性，导致机器人示教极度稀缺。以自我为中心的人类示教是可扩展的替代方案，但已有方法为便于迁移而简化人类运动，恰好丢弃了此类任务依赖的细粒度耦合结构。

Method: 构建完整系统 DexRoam：(1) 无追踪器采集系统，仅用消费级 VR 头显与头戴立体相机，无需外部相机或运动捕捉设备，实现可扩展的全身人类操作示教采集；(2) 三阶段显式对齐——本体对齐、动作语义对齐、时间对齐，将采集动作映射至机器人动作空间，保留细粒度全身运动，使人类与机器人示教可被标准 VLA 策略联合学习。

Result: 真实世界实验在多种 VLA 骨干上显示，人类示教在不同训练范式下均一致提升策略学习：GR00T N1.7 平均成功率由 29% 提升至 56%，pi0.5 由 32% 提升至 57%；且仅用一半机器人示教即可匹配纯机器人训练效果。消融实验证实每个对齐阶段均为必要。

Conclusion: 在保留细粒度运动结构的前提下，人类示教对可扩展的全身移动操作具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation requires continuous coordination of locomotion, whole-body motion, and finger-level dexterity within a single trajectory, creating a severe robot demonstration bottleneck. Egocentric human demonstrations offer a scalable alternative, but prior approaches ease the transfer by simplifying human motion, discarding exactly the fine-grained, coupled structure such tasks depend on. We present DexRoam, a complete system for learning mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation from human demonstrations, in which whole-body motion remains continuous and coupled throughout the human-to-robot transfer process. To enable scalable collection of whole-body human manipulation demonstrations, we develop a tracker-free capture system using only a consumer VR headset and a head-mounted stereo camera, without external cameras or motion trackers. We then perform three explicit alignment stages---embodiment, action-semantic, and temporal---to map captured motion into the robot action space, preserving fine-grained whole-body motion and allowing human and robot demonstrations to be jointly learned by standard VLA policies. Real-world experiments with different VLA backbones show that human demonstrations consistently improve policy learning across training paradigms, raising average success from 29% to 56% on GR00T N1.7 and from 32% to 57% on pi0.5, while matching robot-only training with half the robot demonstrations. Ablations confirm that each alignment stage is necessary. These results highlight the potential of human demonstrations for scalable whole-body mobile manipulation with preserved fine-grained motion structure.
